Through controlled experiments with split output architectures on MNIST, the authors demonstrate that auxiliary head compatibility enables knowledge transfer even across architectures, and they develop a theoretical framework predicting when subliminal learning succeeds or fails.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior work attributed subliminal learning (knowledge/bias transfer via task-unrelated distillation) to shared or closely matched teacher-student initialization, but this explanation was incomplete. Understanding when and how subliminal learning occurs is critical for controlling knowledge transfer in neural networks.

Method: Controlled MNIST experiments with split output architecture (auxiliary head for task-unrelated noise signals, class head for classification). Systematic testing includes random initialization, layer removal/addition, and architecture conversion (MLP-to-CNN). Theoretical analysis to explain mechanism and derive failure bounds.

Result: Subliminal learning occurs even with random initialization, layer modifications, and cross-architecture transfer (MLP-to-CNN). Compatible auxiliary heads enable transfer of recoverable teacher signals, aligning student representations with teacher. With compatible class heads, students trained only on noise can match teacher-level performance. Upper bounds on failure conditions were derived.

Conclusion: The paper transforms subliminal learning from a surprising transfer effect into a theoretically grounded mechanism with predictable limits, enabling better understanding and control of knowledge distillation in neural networks.

Abstract: In the context of artificial neural networks, subliminal learning refers to the transfer of task-relevant knowledge or unintended biases from teacher to student models through distillation on task-unrelated input$\unicode{x2013}$output pairs. Prior explanations tie this effect to shared or closely matched teacher$\unicode{x2013}$student initialization. We show that a closely matched initialization is not necessary. Instead, subliminal learning is governed by compatible output heads. Using a controlled MNIST setting, we split outputs into an auxiliary head (for auxiliary, task-unrelated noise signals) and a class head (for classification) to demonstrate subliminal learning occurs$\unicode{x2014}$even when we randomly initialize hidden layers and remove layers, add new layers, or change the architecture (MLP-to-CNN). Compatible auxiliary heads enable transfer of a recoverable teacher signal, bringing the student's representations closer to the teacher's. When the class heads remain compatible as well, students trained only on task-unrelated noise can approach, and in favorable regimes match, teacher-level task performance. Our setting enables us to develop a theory that explains the mechanism of subliminal learning and to derive upper bounds on when subliminal learning fails. Together, our results turn subliminal learning from a surprising transfer effect into a theoretically grounded mechanism with predictable limits.

</details>


### [149] [Valid and Expressive Copulas for Irregular Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23632)
*Christian Klötergens,Tom Hanika,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CopFITi is a novel copula model for irregular multivariate time series forecasting that combines normalizing flows with Gaussian Mixture Copula, achieving marginalization-consistency and state-of-the-art performance in joint density modeling.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Irregular multivariate time series (IMTS) forecasting requires modeling both accurate univariate marginals and flexible joint dependency structures, but existing approaches often fail to decouple these components effectively.

Method: Combines normalizing flows (for univariate marginals) with Gaussian Mixture Copula (for joint dependency structure).

Result: Copula-based approaches that decouple marginals from joint yield better marginal models than architectures directly fitting the full joint.

Conclusion: CopFITi establishes a new state of the art in joint IMTS density modeling, being the first IMTS copula that is marginalization-consistent by construction.

Abstract: We introduce CopFITi, a copula model for probabilistic forecasting of irregular multivariate time series (IMTS). Our model combines the expressivity of normalizing flows for univariate marginals with the consistency and flexibility of a Gaussian Mixture Copula for the joint dependency structure. Our experiments show that copula-based approaches, which decouple the marginals from the joint, yield better marginal models than architectures that directly fit the full joint. With CopFITi, we propose the first IMTS copula that is marginalization-consistent by construction and establish a new state of the art in joint IMTS density modeling.

</details>


### [150] [It's the humans, not the data: Geopolitical bias in LLMs originates in post-training, amplified by the language of the prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23825)
*Stuart Bladon,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Geopolitical bias in language models originates from post-training (alignment) rather than pre-training, shifting models toward the country/region of their developers.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: It has been assumed that geopolitical bias originates from pre-training data; this assumption needed empirical validation.

Method: Tested seven open-weight LLM pairs (base vs. chat models) from seven labs using a paired-scenario forced-choice probe across 28 country pairs in English, French, and Chinese.

Result: Six of seven labs showed geopolitical bias shifts toward the developer's country/region after post-training. Qwen 2.5 exhibited the largest shift (18x in odds). Bias magnitude depended on the prompt language (e.g., Mistral became pro-France only under French prompting).

Conclusion: Geopolitical preferences in LLMs are actively shaped during post-training rather than inherited from pre-training data, necessitating greater transparency, auditing, and oversight of alignment processes.

Abstract: It has generally been assumed that geopolitical bias in language models originates from the training data used during the pre-training phase. We tested seven open-weight LLM pairs consisting of the base model (pre-training only) and the chat model (pre-training and post-training) from seven labs on a paired-scenario forced-choice probe over 28 country pairs in English, French, and Chinese, and found that geopolitical bias originates in post-training rather than in pre-training. Across seven AI labs, six showed shifts in the direction associated with the country or region of the model developer after post-training. This shift is strongest in Alibaba's Qwen 2.5: while the base is neutral on China-favourability (-0.15 log-odds, p=0.15), the post-trained chat variant is at +2.91 (p<10^-4), an 18x shift in odds. We also observe shifts in biases toward other countries across all models. Additionally, the magnitude of this shift depends on the language used to prompt the model: the French-made Mistral becomes pro-France only under French prompting (FR-EN shift +1.91, p<10^-4). These findings suggest that geopolitical preferences in language models are not simply inherited from large-scale internet data but are actively shaped during post-training, highlighting the need for greater transparency, auditing, and oversight of alignment processes that influence how models represent nations, cultures, and political perspectives.

</details>


### [151] [Leveraging Foundation Models for Causal Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23861)
*Aneesh Komanduri,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FM-CGM框架，将预训练基础模型（如大语言模型和扩散模型）融入因果生成建模，实现端到端视觉因果推理。该框架通过概念提取、操纵和反事实生成三阶段流程，结合CSG语义引导机制，在零样本设置下完成因果发现、干预和反事实生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果生成建模方法将因果约束融入生成模型训练，但缺乏统一框架来发挥预训练基础模型的零样本推理能力。

Method: FM-CGM框架包含三个核心组件（概念提取器、概念操纵器、反事实生成器），利用大型推理模型进行因果推理，文本到图像扩散模型进行生成；另提出因果语义引导(CSG)机制，通过交叉注意力确保语义干预传播至后继概念同时保留不变区域。

Result: 实验表明该方法能够识别合理的因果结构，并生成忠实于因果机制的反事实图像。

Conclusion: FM-CGM successfully unifies foundation models with causal reasoning, enabling end-to-end zero-shot causal discovery, intervention, and counterfactual generation with semantic consistency.

Abstract: Causal generative modeling is essential for developing reliable and transparent AI systems capable of counterfactual reasoning. While existing approaches focus on integrating causal constraints during the training of generative models, they often lack a unified framework to leverage the zero-shot reasoning capabilities of pretrained foundation models. We introduce FM-CGM, a modular framework for end-to-end visual causal reasoning using pretrained foundation models. FM-CGM formalizes the causal pipeline through three core components: a concept extractor, a concept manipulator, and a counterfactual generator. By leveraging a large reasoning model for causal inference and a text-to-image diffusion model for generation, our approach enables zero-shot causal discovery, intervention, and counterfactual generation. We then develop Causal Semantic Guidance (CSG), a cross-attention-based mechanism that ensures semantic interventions propagate to descendant concepts while preserving invariant regions. We empirically show that our approach can identify plausible causal structures and is suitable for faithful counterfactual image generation.

</details>


### [152] [LLMs as Noisy Channels: A Shannon Perspective on Model Capacity and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23901)
*Xu Ouyang,Deyi Liu,Yuhang Cai,Jing Liu,Yuan Yang,Chen Zheng,Thomas Hartvigsen,Yiyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Shannon Scaling Law，利用香农-哈特利定理将LLM训练建模为噪声信道上的信息传输过程，通过将模型参数映射为信道带宽、训练token映射为信号功率，揭示了足够信噪比(SNR)的重要性。理论预测：仅扩展模型规模或数据而不维持足够SNR会导致性能从单调改善转变为U型退化。实验在Pythia和OLMo2上验证，通过高斯噪声、量化及数学/QA/代码任务微调扰动，Shannon Scaling Law一致优于经典scaling law，在≤6.9B模型≤180B token数据上拟合后，可预测12B模型在307B token时的性能(pooled R²=0.847)，而单调基线方法失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing monotonic power law scaling laws fail to explain critical non-monotonic phenomena: catastrophic overtraining and quantization-induced degradation where performance deteriorates with increased compute.

Method: Shannon-Hartley theorem application: mapping model parameters to channel bandwidth and training tokens to signal power, analyzing SNR dynamics during scaling.

Result: Outperforms classical and perturbation-aware scaling laws with strong R² scores; accurately captures loss basins; extrapolates to 12B model (unseen) at 307B tokens with pooled R²=0.847, while monotonic baselines collapse.

Conclusion: Shannon Scaling Law provides a unified framework explaining both monotonic improvement and non-monotonic degradation in LLM training, with strong predictive power (R²=0.847) for unseen model scales.

Abstract: Existing scaling laws for Large Language Models (LLMs), predominantly monotonic power laws, fail to explain emerging non-monotonic phenomena such as catastrophic overtraining and quantization-induced degradation, where performance deteriorates despite increased compute.
  We propose the Shannon Scaling Law, a unified theoretical framework that models LLM training as information transmission over a noisy channel, grounded in the Shannon-Hartley theorem. By mapping model parameters to channel bandwidth and training tokens to signal power, our formulation explicitly captures the interaction between learning signal and intrinsic noise. This perspective reveals a fundamental Shannon capacity for LLMs: scaling model size or data without preserving a sufficient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) inevitably amplifies noise, inducing a transition from monotonic improvement to U-shaped performance degradation.
  We validate our theory through experiments on Pythia and OLMo2 under perturbations, including Gaussian noise, quantization and supervised fine-tuning on math, QA and code tasks. The Shannon Scaling Law consistently outperforms classical scaling laws and recent perturbation-aware laws, achieving strong $R^2$ scores and accurately capturing loss basins missed by prior approaches. It also extrapolates: fitted on $\leq$6.9B Pythia models with $\leq$180B tokens, it predicts the unseen 12B model up to 307B tokens at pooled $R^2{=}0.847$, while monotonic baselines collapse.

</details>


### [153] [Learning Dynamic Stability Landscapes in Synchronization Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23708)
*Christian Nauck,Junyou Zhu,Michael Lindner,Frank Hellmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Proposes learning stability landscapes from graph topology using a novel graph-to-image prediction paradigm (GNN + CNN decoder). Releases datasets of 10,000 graphs with per-node landscape labels. Demonstrates learnability of stability landscapes that exceed conventional network science approaches.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional scalar per-node stability indices provide limited insights into synchronization behavior. Deeper characterization of synchronization robustness is needed.

Method: Graph-to-image prediction paradigm: GNN encodes graph topology, CNN decoder renders per-node image-like landscapes, learned end-to-end. Two datasets of 10,000 graphs each (20 and 100 nodes) released based on oscillator model.

Result: Good in-distribution accuracy achieved. Model generalizes across graph sizes and to realistic power grid topologies.

Conclusion: Stability landscapes are learnable from topology and open new avenues for moving beyond scalar stability indices in biology, neuroscience, and power grids.

Abstract: The robustness of synchronization is typically characterized by scalar, per-node stability indices whose dependence on topology is studied via network science or graph neural networks (GNNs). We propose a novel upstream task, learning stability landscapes, which provide deeper insights into synchronization behavior and from which many such scalar indices can be derived. Crucially, we pioneer a graph-to-image prediction paradigm: learning image-like landscapes as per-node targets directly from graph topology, a formulation we are not aware of having been established elsewhere in the literature. To support this task, we release two datasets of 10,000 graphs each at 20 and 100 nodes with per-node landscape labels, based on a conceptual oscillator model, capturing power grid synchronization behavior. A GNN encodes topology and a CNN decoder renders per-node images, learned end-to-end with good in-distribution accuracy, generalizing across graph sizes and to realistic power grid topologies. This demonstrates that stability landscapes, while beyond the reach of conventional network science, are learnable from topology and open new avenues for moving beyond scalar stability indices in biology, neuroscience, and power grids.

</details>


### [154] [Approaching I/O-optimality for Approximate Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23751)
*Pál András Papp,Aleksandros Sobczyk,Anastasios Zouzias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM注意力机制的I/O复杂度进行优化，通过借鉴近似注意力框架，将传统O(n²)的I/O开销降低至接近O(n)的线性水平，并证明了对应lower bounds验证算法接近I/O最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FlashAttention及其变体的I/O开销与n呈二次关系，但trivial lower bound仅为Ω(nd)，理论上应可优化。

Method: 借鉴Alman和Song的近似注意力框架，开发了I/O高效算法。

Result: 在大多数参数配置下，I/O成本仅接近线性依赖于n，相比现有方法有显著改善。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法接近I/O最优，解决了理论计算复杂度与实际实现之间的gap。

Abstract: We revisit the I/O complexity of attention in large language models. Given query-key-value matrices $Q,K,V\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}$, and a machine with fast memory size $M$, the goal is to compute the "attention matrix" $A=\text{softmax}(Q K ^{\top}/\sqrt{d}) V$ with the minimal number of data transfers between fast and slow memory. Existing methods in the literature, most notably FlashAttention and its variants, incur an I/O cost that depends quadratically on $n$, while a trivial lower bound only requires $Ω(nd)$ I/O's to read the inputs and write the output. In this work, we present a technique for computing attention where the I/O cost only depends almost-linearly on $n$ in most parameter regimes. This is achieved by developing I/O-efficient algorithms inspired by the recent approximate attention framework of Alman and Song. We also prove corresponding lower bounds in each parameter regime to show that our algorithms are indeed close to I/O-optimal.

</details>


### [155] [SeedER: Seed-and-Expand Retrieval from Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23753)
*Hamed Shirzad,Frederik Wenkel,Dominique Beaini,Danica J. Sutherland,Emmanuel Noutahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SeedER是一种知识图谱检索框架，通过迭代、低成本的扩展策略解决多跳组合查询难题。先用密集检索播种核心节点，再用强化学习训练的图感知策略选择性扩展，在紧凑候选集上实现高召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱结构不规则，传统方法面临挑战：自我图扩展增长迅速，密集嵌入方法难以处理多跳组合查询，现有基于智能体的图探索方法虽然表达力强但在大规模检索中成本过高。

Method: SeedER采用两阶段策略：1）使用轻量级密集检索和实体检索播种核心节点集；2）通过强化学习训练的图感知策略选择性扩展节点集。该策略将全局推理分解为可重用的局部决策，从而有效控制扩展成本。此外，论文从组合泛化和图约束子模优化角度建立了理论优势。

Result: 实验表明，SeedER在紧凑候选集上显著提升了召回率，优于强基线的密集检索和图增强方法。

Conclusion: SeedER提出了一种可扩展的知识图谱检索框架，通过种子节点选择和图感知策略扩展相结合，在紧凑的候选集上实现高召回率，为知识密集型推理系统提供了有效的第一阶段检索器。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) offer a rich representation for relational knowledge, but their irregular structure makes retrieval challenging: ego-graph expansion grows rapidly, and dense embedding methods struggle with multi-hop compositional queries. Existing agent-based graph exploration approaches, while expressive, are often too expensive for large-scale retrieval. We introduce SeedER (Seed-and-Expand Retrieval), a retrieval framework that explicitly leverages KG structure through iterative, low-cost expansion. SeedER first seeds a compact set of core nodes using lightweight dense and entity-based retrieval, then selectively expands this set via a learned graph-aware policy trained with reinforcement learning. This design decomposes global reasoning into reusable local decisions, enabling efficient discovery of query-relevant nodes while tightly controlling expansion cost. We show theoretical limitations of dense retrieval on compositional graph queries, and establish advantages of SeedER from both compositional generalization and graph-constrained submodular optimization perspectives. Empirically, SeedER substantially improves recall with compact candidate sets over strong dense and graph-augmented baselines, making it an effective first-stage retriever for knowledge-intensive reasoning systems.

</details>


### [156] [Debiased Negative Mining Improves Out-of-distribution Detection with Pre-trained Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23797)
*Bo Peng,Jie Lu,Guangquan Zhang,Zhen Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）的分布外检测（OOD）问题，提出通过修正负样本标签的采样偏差来提升检测性能。核心创新是将去偏负样本挖掘转化为基于ID标签和无标注数据的蒙特卡洛采样问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM后验OOD检测方法依赖启发式规则从无标注数据中挖掘负样本标签，但存在严重的"假负样本"问题，导致VLM的OOD检测能力未被充分释放。

Method: 建立理论框架，通过间接近似负样本标签分布来修正采样偏差；将去偏负样本挖掘问题转化为基于ID标签和无标注野生数据的蒙特卡洛采样。

Result: 在多种OOD检测设置下取得新的最优性能（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 所提出的去偏负样本挖掘方法能有效提升基于预训练VLM的OOD检测性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Aiming at identifying unexpected inputs from unknown classes, out-of-distribution (OOD) detection has emerged as a pivotal approach to enhancing the reliability of machine learning models. This paper focuses on the burgeoning paradigm of post-hoc OOD detection with pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs), where a popular pipeline is to detect OOD inputs by examining their affinities between ID labels and negative labels, i.e., those semantically different from ID labels. Due to the unavailability of target OOD labels, existing works predominantly rely on heuristic rules to mine negative labels from unlabeled wild corpus data. Despite the empirical success, we argue that the power of VLM-based OOD detection has yet to be fully unleashed since the notorious false negative problem is far from addressed in the literature. With this motivation, we are interested in addressing the challenge of mining true negative labels for OOD scoring. To this end, we develop a theoretical framework for correcting the sampling bias of negatives labels by indirectly approximating the distribution of negative labels. Perhaps surprisingly, we show that the debiased negative mining can be naturally converted into Monte-Carlo sampling based on ID labels and the unlabeled wild corpus data. Extensive experiments empirically manifest that our method establishes a new state-of-the-art in a variety of OOD detection setups. Code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/60pen9/Debiased-Negative-Mining-Improves-OOD-Detection-with-Pre-trained-VLMs}{\textcolor{red}{here}}.

</details>


### [157] [Training-Free Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23872)
*Lizhang Chen,Jonathan Li,Chen Liang,Ni Lao,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无需训练的循环transformer方法，将预训练模型的中间层块在推理时循环应用，通过ODE近似的细化视角替代朴素重应用，在多个模型家族上验证了性能提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环transformer方法需要端到端训练循环结构，该研究首次将循环机制直接改装到预训练模型上实现推理时增强。

Method: 采用轻量级推理时包装器，对冻结检查点的连续中间层块进行循环应用。将pre-norm transformer块视为ODE上的前向欧拉步骤，将循环视为同一近似的细化，用较小的阻尼子步骤替代一次大更新。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Instruct上MMLU-Pro提升2.64pp，Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct上CommonsenseQA提升1.14pp，Moonlight-16B-A3B-Instruct上OpenBookQA提升1.20pp。朴素层重应用通常导致性能下降，循环应用策略至关重要。

Conclusion: 该方法在七种密集型、稀疏MoE和MLA+MoE模型家族中均有效提升性能，无需额外训练即可增强预训练模型能力。

Abstract: We introduce training-free looped transformers, in which a lightweight inference-time wrapper loops a contiguous mid-stack block of layers of a frozen checkpoint without additional fine-tuning, continued training, or architectural changes. Unlike prior looped transformer methods that train with the looped structure end-to-end, we retrofit recurrence onto pretrained models at test time. We show that naive block reapplication usually degrades performance, highlighting the importance of the loop application strategy. Motivated by viewing a pre-norm transformer block as a forward Euler step on an ODE, we instead treat looping as a refinement of the same approximation, replacing one large update with smaller damped sub-steps. Across seven dense, sparse MoE, and MLA+MoE model families, our method improves Qwen3-4B-Instruct by +2.64 pp on MMLU-Pro, Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct by +1.14 pp on CommonsenseQA, and Moonlight-16B-A3B-Instruct by +1.20 pp on OpenBookQA.

</details>


### [158] [Complete-muE: Optimal Hyperparameter Transfer and Scaling for MoE Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23893)
*Hongwu Peng,Ohiremen Dibua,Yuanjun Xiong,Yifan Gong,Jianming Zhang,Yan Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Complete-muE是一个超参数迁移框架，通过双桥系统解决从Dense FFN到各种MoE配置的超参数迁移问题。Bridge I处理Dense到Dense MoE的迁移，Bridge II处理Dense MoE到Sparse MoE的迁移。核心实践准则是"一次调参，迁移全部"——在单个Dense参考模型上调优后，可高效迁移到所有MoE配置，避免昂贵的超参数搜索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing hyperparameter transfer tools have critical limitations: μP requires fixed architecture, while SDE requires fixed per-step token count. Neither can directly solve transfer problems in MoE setups where Dense to MoE transitions or total expert scaling involve simultaneous changes in both architecture and tokens per expert. This gap prevents efficient hyperparameter transfer when scaling model capacity to MoE configurations.

Method: Complete-muE employs a two-bridge transfer system: (1) Bridge I maps dense FFN to Dense MoE using active-width μP with normalized router scale; (2) Bridge II maps Dense MoE to sparse MoE via activated-expert scaling, where first-order SDE LR/WD correction cancels while bounded residual σ₀ shift remains. The framework handles architectural and token-level variations that existing methods (μP or SDE) cannot address independently.

Result: Experiments on language model and diffusion model pretraining demonstrate that Complete-muE yields relatively stable hyperparameter optima across model architectures and parameter counts. The observed drift is minor and consistent with the non-strict SDE behavior of Bridge II. Hyperparameters tuned on a single dense reference transfer near-optimally to all MoE configurations, enabling MoE models to achieve accelerated convergence speedup over dense models when scaling capacity.

Conclusion: Complete-muE enables efficient hyperparameter transfer from dense models to various MoE configurations through a two-bridge system, achieving the practical principle of "tune dense once, transfer to all". This framework supports diverse architecture changes (activated experts, capacity, granularity, shared/group-balanced hybrids) and general transformer changes (width/depth, batch size, duration), accelerating MoE model convergence without costly hyperparameter search.

Abstract: We propose Complete-muE, a framework which targets hyperparameter transfer across dense FFN and any Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) setups in transformer blocks. Existing tools such as $μ$P (requires fixed architectue) or SDE (requires fixed per-step token count) cannot directly solve the hyperparameter transfer problem in MoE setups because Dense to MoE transfer or MoE total experts scaling changes both architecture and tokens per expert. Complete-muE solves this challenge with a two-bridge system: Bridge~I maps between dense FFN and Dense MoE by active-width $μ$P with a normalized router scale. Bridge~II maps between Dense MoE and sparse MoE by activated-expert scaling, where the first-order SDE LR/WD correction cancels while a bounded residual $σ_0$ shift remains. The resulting transfer rule, which we term as Complete muE, covers changes in activated experts, total capacity, granularity, and shared/group-balanced hybrids for MoE models as well as network width/depth, batch size, and duration changes for general Transformer models. Extensive language model and diffusion model pretraining experiments confirm that complete-muE yields relatively stable hyperparameter optima across model architectures and parameter counts -- with only minor drift consistent with the non-strict SDE behavior of Bridge~II. In practice this drift is small enough that hyperparameters tuned on a single dense reference transfer near-optimally to all MoE configurations -- \emph{tune dense once, transfer to all} is the practical recipe at the core of Complete-muE. This enables MoE models to achieve accelerated convergence speedup over dense models when scaling model capacity without costly hyperparameter search.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [An AI-Driven Framework for Energy-Efficient Environmental Monitoring in Smart Cities Using Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22824)
*Yichen Liu,Imam Akintomiwa Akinlade,Xiaochong Jiang,Wenting Yang,Shiqi Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: An AI-driven framework leveraging TinyML edge devices and context-aware adaptive decision-making to dynamically activate environmental sensors based on spatiotemporal conditions and energy constraints, achieving significant energy reduction and extended sensor lifespan in smart city deployments.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large-scale smart sensor deployments in smart cities face three critical challenges: excessive energy consumption, redundant data collection, and limited sensor lifespan due to static, continuous operation.

Method: The framework employs TinyML-enabled edge devices with context-aware adaptive decision-making, using a hierarchical Edge Intelligence architecture. Sensors are dynamically activated via a utility function that considers real-time environmental conditions, sensor location, and remaining battery lifespan.

Result: City-scale simulations using real multi-sensor environmental traces demonstrate that the proposed mechanism significantly reduces energy consumption and extends sensor lifespan compared to static, periodic, and UCB-based adaptive sensing strategies.

Conclusion: Edge intelligence and adaptive AI techniques demonstrate significant potential for building sustainable and efficient smart city monitoring systems, effectively balancing energy conservation with monitoring coverage.

Abstract: Environmental monitoring is a crucial component of the smart city infrastructure. It enables informed decision making which enhances sustainability, public health and urban planning. However, the large-scale deployments of the smart sensors have raised concerns on excessive energy consumption and redundant data collection as well as limited sensor lifespan. To resolve these issues, we present an AI-driven framework for energy-efficient environmental monitoring in smart cities utilizing edge intelligence. Our proposed framework leverages TinyML-enabled edge devices and context-aware adaptive decision-making in order to dynamically activate the sensors based on the spatiotemporal conditions, environmental statistics and energy constraints. The sensors will be dynamically activated based on a utility function that takes in factors such as real-time environmental conditions, sensor location, and remaining battery lifespan. Our framework will reduce unnecessary sensing and communication while maintaining high coverage for monitoring. We introduce a hierarchical Edge Intelligence architecture to support deployments in city-wide scales. We conducted evaluation using a city-scale simulation driven by real multi-sensor environmental traces, which demonstrates that the proposed mechanism significantly reduces energy consumption and extends sensor lifespan when compared to static, periodic, and UCB-based adaptive sensing strategies. The results highlight the potential of edge intelligence and adaptive AI techniques for building sustainable and efficient smart city monitoring systems.

</details>


### [160] [Mathematical Foundations for Peer-to-Peer Lattice Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22832)
*Danil Gorinevski*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Five propositions on synchronous peer-to-peer computation on a grid graph in $\mathbb{Z}^2$.
  \textbf{Proposition~1} gives three lower bounds: a transport-work bound $\sum_i a_i \ell_i \ge W_1(μ,ν)$, a completion-depth bound $D_{\min} \ge r_μ$ on the support radius, and a compressive-reduction edge bound $|E'| \ge \operatorname{St}_G(\operatorname{supp}(μ)\cup\{x_\star\})$. A negative result: for corner-sink dimension-order routing, the sink-trunk route-load functional has variance $Θ(f(1-f)P^2)$ under i.i.d.\ Bernoulli activation, refuting $O(fP^{3/2})$ concentration.
  \textbf{Proposition~2}: under an $αβγ$ collective-communication cost model and a sparse-activation model, the grid-to-cluster latency ratio improves monotonically as $f_{\mathrm{act}}$ shrinks whenever the cluster's fixed overhead dominates the grid's geometric constant.
  \textbf{Proposition~3}: a sufficient algebraic criterion for schedule-independent reduction semantics is that update rules decompose into a local map and an abelian-monoid merge, as a product-preserving functor from the Lawvere theory of commutative monoids into a hardware-state category. Under an idealised non-interfering scheduler this yields a wall-clock bound; the criterion is PCC-certifiable.
  \textbf{Proposition~4} bounds the conditional expected route length under i.i.d.\ site failure in the subcritical regime $δ< p_c^{\mathrm{site}}(\mathbb{Z}^2)$ by an additive detour term, via Aizenman--Barsky cluster-size decay and a boundary-length bound.
  \textbf{Proposition~5} augments the grid with $k$ long-range Watts--Strogatz shortcuts per node, collapsing typical shortest-path length from $Θ(\sqrt{P})$ to $O(\log P)$; percolation is placed in the mean-field universality class (rigorous for the $1$-D-ring base, conjectural for the $2$-D-grid base).

</details>


### [161] [KPI2KVI: A Multi Agent Workflow for Calculating Key Value Indicators from Service Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22825)
*Masoud Shokrnezhad,Tarik Taleb,Yan Chen,Qize Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: KPI2KVI是一种基于LLM的多智能体工作流工具，可将自然语言服务描述自动转换为可计算的KVI估计值，解决了传统KVI计算依赖人工、不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In many domains, KVIs are difficult to compute in practice because they require selecting relevant KVI categories, defining measurable KPIs, collecting KPI values, and applying consistent calculation logic — all typically performed manually and inconsistently from unstructured service documentation.

Method: A deterministic multi-agent workflow powered by LLMs that: (i) elicits missing service context, (ii) extracts and finalizes relevant KVI categories from a taxonomy, (iii) generates service-specific KPIs with units and descriptions, (iv) collects KPI values through interactive dialogue with intelligent estimation for unavailable values, and (v) computes interval-valued KVI outputs (minimum, exact, maximum) with traceable explanations.

Result: Simulations with representative service descriptions demonstrate that KPI2KVI consistently produces complete end-to-end mapping from description to KVI intervals and provides transparent calculation narratives.

Conclusion: KPI2KVI produces complete end-to-end mappings from service descriptions to KVI intervals, with transparent calculation narratives supporting post hoc auditing and interactive advisory queries. Simulations confirm the tool's consistency and utility.

Abstract: Key Value Indicators (KVIs) provide a decision oriented view of a service by summarizing how operational performance translates into stakeholder value, risk, and outcomes. However, in many domains KVIs are difficult to compute in practice because they require selecting relevant KVI categories, defining measurable Key Performance Indicators (KPIs), collecting KPI values, and applying consistent calculation logic, all of which is typically performed manually and inconsistently from unstructured service documentation. This paper presents KPI2KVI, a tool that transforms a natural language service description into computed KVI estimates by orchestrating a deterministic multi agent workflow powered by Large Language Models (LLMs) that (i) elicits missing service context, (ii) extracts and finalizes relevant KVI categories from a taxonomy, (iii) generates service specific KPIs with units and descriptions, (iv) collects KPI values through an interactive dialogue and also supports intelligent estimation for KPI values that are unavailable, and (v) computes interval valued KVI outputs (minimum, exact, maximum) with traceable explanations for each KVI code. Simulations with representative service descriptions demonstrate that KPI2KVI consistently produces a complete end to end mapping from description to KVI intervals and provides transparent calculation narratives that support post hoc auditing and interactive advisory queries.

</details>


### [162] [Monte Cimone v3: Where RISC-V Stands in High-Performance Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22831)
*Emanuele Venieri,Simone Manoni,Giacomo Madella,Federico Proverbio,Federico Ficarelli,Luca Benini,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Monte Cimone v3 (MCv3)是第三代RISC-V高性能计算集群，采用SOPHGO Sophon SG2044处理器。评测结果表明，SG2044单核性能提升超过1倍，能效达3.08GFLOPs/W（较MCv1提升10倍），在16核配置下性能分别为Intel Sapphire Rapids的46%和NVIDIA Grace的91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RISC-V架构在高性能计算(HPC)领域的应用探索需要一个专门的测试平台，以评估其性能、能效和可扩展性。

Method: 使用HPL和STREAM基准测试结合功耗测量对MCv3进行性能表征，并与Intel Xeon Platinum 8480+(Sapphire Rapids)和NVIDIA Grace CPU Superchip进行对比评估。

Result: SG2044相比SG2042单核性能提升超过1倍，扩展性改善；能效达3.08GFLOPs/W；在16核峰值效率点下性能分别达到Intel Sapphire Rapids的46%和NVIDIA Grace的91%。

Conclusion: MCv3通过采用SG2044处理器实现了重大的性能突破，能效比提升10倍，在能效方面可与x86-64和Arm服务器相媲美，且在峰值效率点(16核)下性能可达Intel Sapphire Rapids的46%和NVIDIA Grace的91%。

Abstract: The Monte Cimone project provides a RISC-V testbed for High-Performacne Computing cluster. This paper presents Monte Cimone v3 (MCv3), the third iteration of the Monte Cimone RISC-V HPC cluster, integrating the SOPHGO Sophon SG2044 processor, an evolution of the SG2042 used in MCv2. We characterize MCv3 using HPL and STREAM benchmarks coupled with power measurements, and compare it against two reference platforms: the Intel Xeon Platinum 8480+(Sapphire Rapids) and the NVIDIA Grace CPU Superchip. Our results show that the SG2044 more than doubles single-core performance and improves scalability compared to SG2042. MCv3 achieves an energy efficiency of 3.08GFLOPs/W which improves of 10x w.r.t. MCv1 and is in the range of x86-64 and Arm servers. On pure performance when normalized on the SIMD/Vector length MCv3 on its peak efficiency point (16 cores) achieves 46% performance of Intel Sapphire Rapids server and 91% performance of NVIDIA Grace CPU superchip.

</details>


### [163] [ObjectCache: Layerwise Object-Storage Retrieval for KV Cache Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22850)
*Yu Zhu,Aditya Dhakal,Yunming Xiao,Dejan Milojicic,Gustavo Alonso*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ObjectCache是一种将LLM推理中的KV缓存卸载至S3对象存储的方案，通过联合优化存储协议和传输调度来隐藏远程存储的访问延迟，在长上下文场景下性能接近本地DRAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理中的Prefix KV缓存虽能减少首 token 时间(TTFT)，但累积的KV缓存常超出GPU内存和本地DRAM容量，导致系统不得不依赖昂贵的远程DRAM池，增加了集群规模和成本。

Method: 提出ObjectCache，联合设计存储协议和数据传输调度：存储服务器按GPU消费顺序交付KV缓存数据，在并发请求间重叠数据传输与计算。基于100 Gbps RoCE集群原型实现，集成了NIXL、Ceph RGW和DAOS。

Result: 实验表明：64K上下文场景下仅比本地DRAM增加5.6%延迟；4K上下文场景增加56-75ms延迟；在共享带宽限制下，调度器相比平等带宽分配策略降低TTFT 1.2-1.8倍。

Conclusion: ObjectCache通过将KV缓存卸载至S3兼容对象存储，在64K上下文下仅增加5.6%延迟，4K上下文下增加56-75ms，同时调度器在带宽受限下可降低1.2-1.8倍TTFT。

Abstract: Prefix KV caching has become a key mechanism in LLM serving: it reduces time to first token (TTFT) by avoiding redundant computation across requests that share a prefix (i.e., the system prompt). However, the accumulated KV cache is often larger than what GPU memory and local DRAM can hold. To preserve latency, current systems keep the KV cache in remote DRAM pools, increasing serving-cluster size and cost. In this paper, we explore a different approach: storing the KV cache in S3-compatible object storage so that capacity is no longer the constraint, while minimizing the impact on TTFT. We propose ObjectCache, which co-designs the storage protocol and transfer schedule so that the storage server delivers KV cache data in the order the GPU consumes it, overlapping data transfer with compute across concurrent requests. We prototype ObjectCache on a 100 Gbps RoCE cluster with NIXL (an inference library that abstracts storage and memory), Ceph RGW (an Object Gateway for clusters), and DAOS (an open source storage system). For 64K contexts, common in today's systems, ObjectCache adds only 5.6\% latency over local DRAM; for 4K contexts, where less compute is available to mask transfer, ObjectCache adds 56--75\,ms over the optimal local layerwise baseline. Under shared bandwidth caps, our scheduler reduces added TTFT by 1.2--1.8x compared with equal bandwidth sharing.

</details>


### [164] [Budgeted Dynamic Trace Structures for Token-Efficient Sequential Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22879)
*Faruk Alpay,Levent Sarioglu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对顺序计算产生的长轨迹维护问题，提出BDTS框架，通过多种数据结构和压缩技术的组合，在显式字节/token预算约束下高效管理有根轨迹图和追加式历史，实验验证了其在图构建、遍历和压缩方面的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序计算产生的长轨迹包含嵌套分支、状态转换、文本载荷和早期执行摘要，在资源受限场景下需要有效维护。

Method: 提出预算化动态跟踪结构(BDTS)数据框架，融合状态过滤可达性、游标分页、软上限最近日志、引用计数观察键、增量覆盖、bounded cost缓存和摘要+后缀压缩等多种技术；提供形式化不变量和渐近边界；实现Rust原型并含可复现基准测试。

Result: 10k-40k顶点规模下建图耗时0.58-2.72ms，遍历所有后继耗时0.24-1.42ms；350k-2.71M token压缩至1,048-4,120 token；三种公开模型目标测试中3359-3360 token压缩至432-433 token。

Conclusion: BDTS框架为顺序计算产生的复杂长轨迹提供了一种有效的预算管理方案，实验验证其在不同规模数据下均能实现高效的图构建、遍历和压缩性能。

Abstract: Sequential computation increasingly produces long traces containing nested branches, status transitions, textual payloads, and compact summaries of earlier execution. This paper introduces budgeted dynamic trace structures (BDTS), a data-structural framework for maintaining rooted trace graphs and append-only histories under an explicit byte or token budget. BDTS combines status-filtered reachability, cursor pagination, soft-capped recency logs, reference-counted observation keys, delta overlays, bounded cost caches, and summary-plus-suffix compaction. We give formal invariants, asymptotic bounds, and an ancillary Rust implementation with reproducible benchmarks. Across synthetic traces with 10,000-40,000 vertices, the prototype builds graphs in 0.58-2.72 ms, enumerates all descendants in 0.24-1.42 ms, and compacts histories of 350k-2.71M approximate tokens to 1,048-4,120 approximate tokens. Tokenizer and forward measurements with three public model targets reduce 3,359-3,360 trace tokens to 432-433 tokens.

</details>


### [165] [Orbax: Distributed Checkpointing with JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23066)
*Colin Gaffney,Shutong Li,Daniel Ng,Anastasia Petrushkina,Niket Kumar,Adam Cogdell,Mridul Sahu,Yaning Liang,Nikhil Bansal,Justin Pan,Angel Mau,Abhishek Agrawal,Marco Berlot,Ruoxin Sang,Kiranbir Sodhia,Rakesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Orbax is a modular checkpointing library designed specifically for JAX-based distributed ML systems, offering superior performance (3.5x faster saving, 2x faster loading) compared to PyTorch alternatives while maintaining flexibility across the ML model lifecycle.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JAX has become a leading framework for high-performance distributed ML systems, but its modular design philosophy resulted in a lack of standardized checkpointing solutions. Existing solutions are either not optimized for JAX's architecture or fail to handle the complexities of distributed accelerator systems adequately.

Method: The authors developed Orbax as a JAX-native checkpointing library that abstracts distributed accelerator complexities while providing flexible, user-friendly checkpoint manipulation tools throughout the entire ML model lifecycle.

Result: Orbax demonstrates significant performance improvements over comparable PyTorch-based checkpointing solutions: up to 3.5x faster for save operations and 2x faster for load operations. The library is open source and available on GitHub.

Conclusion: Orbax successfully fills a critical gap in the JAX ecosystem by providing a modular, high-performance checkpointing solution that addresses both technical requirements of distributed systems and practical usability needs of ML practitioners.

Abstract: In a landscape of high-performance distributed ML systems, JAX has emerged as a framework of choice. However, JAX's modular design philosophy leaves it without a standardized checkpointing solution. In this paper, we introduce Orbax, a modular, JAX-native checkpointing library that abstracts the complexities of distributed accelerator systems while also providing flexibility for user-friendly checkpoint manipulations throughout the ML model lifecycle. We demonstrate performance exceeding comparable PyTorch competitors by up to 3.5$\times$ for saving and 2$\times$ for loading. The library is available at https://github.com/google/orbax.

</details>


### [166] [XWind: A Cross-site Router for Large Language Model Inference Serving at Renewable Energy Farms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23348)
*Tella Rajashekhar Reddy,Atharva Deshmukh,Liangcheng Yu,Chaojie Zhang,Mike Shepperd,Rohan Gandhi,Anjaly Parayil,Srinivasan Iyengar,Ajay Manchepalli,Debopam Bhattacherjee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: The paper proposes AI Greenferencing to co-locate modular AI inference infrastructure with wind energy sources, addressing grid strain while leveraging abundant renewables. XWind, a reactive inference router using real-time signals, achieves up to 52-98% latency reduction over baselines in evaluations on a real 64-GPU A100 testbed with Azure production traces.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI power demand is growing rapidly while power grids struggle to keep pace. Grid expansion is costly with long-distance transmission losses, yet renewable energy sources are abundant but geographically mismatched with demand centers.

Method: The paper proposes AI Greenferencing—a model deploying modular AI compute at wind sites—and develops XWind, a lightweight reactive inference router that uses real-time signals (inference latency, KV-cache utilization, queue depth) to dynamically route workloads across wind-powered sites. Feasibility is validated via spatial analysis of wind capacity within 50ms RTT of Azure data centers, combined with site-wise right-sizing and spatial complementarity of wind energy.

Result: XWind reduces P99 end-to-end latency by up to 52% versus the best alternative and up to 98% versus baselines (power-capping, GPU idling), with consistent improvements across workloads, load levels, and GPU generations, while maintaining aggregate fleet utilization comparable to traditional deployments.

Conclusion: AI Greenferencing is feasible and effective: 890+ GW of wind capacity is within viable network distance of Azure data centers, and XWind achieves substantial latency reductions while maintaining fleet utilization parity with traditional deployments.

Abstract: AI power demand is growing at an unprecedented rate while power grids are often ailing and struggle to keep up. Grid expansion comes with high capital expenditure and long-distance transmission losses, yet there is abundant renewable energy at the source, just not matched to demand.
  This paper proposes a complementary AI infrastructure deployment model, AI Greenferencing, that brings modular AI compute to renewable energy sources, focusing on wind, allowing AI footprint expansion, generating local behind-the-meter demand for renewable sites, and helping ease the growing strain on power utilities. Our feasibility analysis shows that 890+ GW of wind capacity lies within 50 ms network round trip time of Azure data centers, and that site-wise right-sizing combined with spatial complementarity of wind energy keeps aggregate fleet utilization on par with traditional deployments.
  To serve inference requests under variable wind power, we build XWind, a lightweight, reactive, and workload-agnostic AI inference router that uses only real-time signals: inference latency, KV-cache utilization, and queue depth, to dynamically configure sites and distribute requests. Evaluated on a real 64-GPU A100 testbed emulating three wind-powered sites with Azure production traces, XWind reduces P99 end-to-end latency by up to 52% over the strongest contender (also our idea) and by up to 98% over baselines such as power-capping and GPU idling, with consistent gains across workload types, load levels, and GPU generations.

</details>


### [167] [AlignedServe: Orchestrating Prefix-aware Batching to Build a High-throughput and Computing-efficient LLM Serving System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23389)
*Fengyao Bai,Hongbin Zhang,Zhitao Chen,Jiangsu Du,Zhiguang Chen,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AlignedServe是一种基于前缀感知批处理的LLM服务框架，通过将KV-cache长度相似的请求分组、使用大规模CPU内存维护飞行中请求，以及GPU-Prefetch-For-GPU架构来减少迭代级和传输延迟瓶颈，实现解码吞吐量1.98倍提升和延迟7.4倍降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务框架通过批处理和调度减少请求级和批次级的气泡，但忽视了每个解码迭代内部的气泡问题。同一迭代中生成的token因依赖不同长度的KV-cache而产生不同成本，长KV-cache的token可能成为瓶颈，延迟下一次迭代。

Method: 提出AlignedServe框架，采用三项核心技术：(1) 前缀感知批处理策略，将KV-cache长度相近的请求分组到同一批次，减少迭代级气泡；(2) 利用大规模CPU内存维护足够多的飞行中请求以支持批处理，并应用批次级调度策略；(3) GPU-Prefetch-For-GPU架构，由一个GPU预取KV-cache供另一个GPU使用，降低CPU到GPU的传输延迟。

Result: 实验表明，AlignedServe在综合和实际应用工作负载下，解码吞吐量提升最高1.98倍，延迟降低最高7.4倍。

Conclusion: AlignedServe通过前缀感知批处理策略和GPU预取架构，在解码吞吐量上实现了最高1.98倍的提升，延迟降低了最高7.4倍，显著优于现有系统。

Abstract: High-throughput inference serving is essential for applications built on large language models (LLMs). Existing serving frameworks reduce request-level and batch-level bubbles through batching and scheduling, but often overlook bubbles within each decode iteration. Tokens generated in the same iteration may incur different costs because they depend on KV caches of different lengths; tokens with long KV caches can become bottlenecks and delay the next iteration. We propose AlignedServe, an LLM serving framework built around prefix-aware batching. It groups requests with similar KV-cache lengths into the same batch to reduce iteration-level bubbles. To support this policy efficiently, AlignedServe uses large CPU memory to maintain sufficient in-flight requests for batching and applies a batch-level scheduling policy to reduce batch-level bubbles. It also introduces a GPU-Prefetch-For-GPU architecture, where one GPU prefetches KV cache for another to reduce CPU-to-GPU transfer latency. Experiments on synthetic and application workloads show that AlignedServe improves decoding throughput by up to 1.98 times and reduces latency by up to 7.4 times over state-of-the-art systems.

</details>


### [168] [Multi-Round Visibility: A Post-Consensus Ordering Layer for DAG-Based BFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23432)
*Pengkun Ren,Dong Hai,Nasrin Sohrabi,Zahir Tari*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Directed acyclic graph (DAG)-based Byzantine Fault-Tolerant (BFT) protocols achieve high throughput by decoupling dissemination from agreement and allowing many vertices to be committed concurrently. This same concurrency, however, weakens ordering evidence at the execution boundary: once units are committed in a shared DAG frontier, their final linearization is driven by traversal or deterministic tie-breaking rather than verifiable structural precedence. Prior fair-ordering designs address ambiguity by collecting or reconstructing transaction-level ordering evidence within the consensus workflow. While effective, this couples ordering with agreement and places ordering logic on the critical path. This paper presents Multi-Round Visibility (MRV), a post-consensus structural ordering layer for DAG-based BFT that reinterprets the committed DAG as an ordering evidence substrate. Committed vertices inherently carry authenticated creator, round, and ancestry metadata, enabling replicas to derive multi-round structural visibility without extra consensus-path messages. MRV accumulates this visibility within a bounded evidence horizon, compares concurrently committed atomic units of fairness (AUFs) after they coexist in the DAG, and derives precedence constraints from Byzantine-robust visibility advantages. When the DAG lacks such constraints, MRV exposes and resolves the remaining ambiguity through deterministic graph completion rather than hiding it inside traversal rules. We implement MRV on a Narwhal/Tusk-based prototype. Evaluation across 5-50 replicas under various fault settings shows MRV preserves the high-throughput regime of the DAG-BFT stack, proving it provides post-consensus structural ordering without burdening the consensus-critical path.

</details>


### [169] [Herring: Parallel Batch-Order-Fairness on DAG-based Blockchain Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23648)
*Marko Putnik,Jérémie Decouchant*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Herring是首个γ-batch-OF DAG BFT协议，其创新点在于将公平层计算并行化分布于已提交的子图上，结合后共识图构建与可靠广播层的缺失边解析，将公平排序从串行瓶颈转变为可并行处理的任务。实验表明该方案在高吞吐场景下性能显著优于FairDAG-RL和DoD，并修复了这些协议中的活性漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEV攻击导致去中心化金融用户每年损失数十亿美元。现有BFT共识协议虽保证总排序但不约束排序选择方式，为对抗性重排留下漏洞。现有batch-OF协议为保证公平性付出了高昂的性能代价：基于领导者的协议将所有公平决策集中于单一副本，而DAG类协议（如FairDAG和DoD）尽管运行在多提议者DAG上却被迫串行执行公平层。

Method: Herring将共识后图构建与DAG可靠广播层的显式缺失边解析相结合，将公平排序从每轮串行瓶颈转变为CPU密集型任务，实现公平层的并行化执行。

Result: 在约10,000 tx/s以内Herring紧跟Narwhal & Tusk的吞吐性能；饱和吞吐比FairDAG-RL高约90%，比DoD-W高约100%；显著降低了饱和状态下的执行延迟。同时发现并修复了FairDAG-RL和DoD中此前未报告的活性漏洞。

Conclusion: Herring实现了首个可并行化的batch-OF公平层，在高吞吐场景下性能显著优于现有方案，同时修复了FairDAG-RL和DoD中的活性漏洞。

Abstract: Transaction ordering attacks extract billions of dollars annually from decentralized finance users in the form of Maximal Extractable Value (MEV). Byzantine Fault-Tolerant (BFT) consensus protocols guarantee total order but place no constraint on how that order is chosen, leaving the door open for adversarial reordering. Batch-order-fairness (batch-OF) protocols close this gap, but existing designs pay a steep performance price for this guarantee. Leader-based protocols such as Themis concentrate all fairness decisions at a single replica, while recent DAG-based proposals FairDAG and DAG of DAGs (DoD) force their fairness layer into strictly serial execution despite running on multi-proposer DAGs.
  We present Herring, the first $γ$-batch-OF DAG BFT protocol whose fairness layer parallelizes the dominant graph construction cost across committed subdags. Herring combines post-consensus graph construction with explicit missing edge resolution piggybacked on the DAG's reliable broadcast layer, a pairing that turns fair ordering from a per-round serial bottleneck into a CPU-bound task. We also uncover previously unreported liveness vulnerabilities in both FairDAG-RL and DoD that a malicious client can trigger to halt the fairness layer indefinitely, and propose patches that we integrate into our reimplementations.
  We implement Herring on top of the Rust implementation of Narwhal \& Tusk and evaluate it against FairDAG-RL, DoD-W, and Themis. Herring tracks the throughput of Narwhal \& Tusk closely up to roughly $10{,}000$\,tx/s, achieves roughly $90\%$ higher saturation throughput than FairDAG-RL and $100\%$ higher than DoD-W, and substantially reduces execution latency at saturation.

</details>


### [170] [AMP: Arc Multi-Proposer Protocol with Bounded Inclusion Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23677)
*Daniel Cason,Gordon Liao,Sergio Mena,Nenad Milošević,Adi Seredinschi,Alessandro Sforzin,João Sousa,Preston Vander Vos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AMP是一种基于Tendermint的多提议者协议，通过引入专门的提议者节点收集和分发用户交易，消除mempool，将传播与共识解耦，从而限制验证者对交易包含和排序的单边控制权，同时提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前区块链系统面临结构性张力：单一验证者在组块时拥有对交易包含和排序的单边控制权。传统市场通过反抢先交易和市场操纵法规来约束这种权力，而监管机构已明确指出区块链金融基础设施缺乏此类规则是一级关注问题。

Method: 在Tendermint共识算法之上构建多提议者架构，将用户交易收集委派给专门的提议者节点。提议者将交易分组为payload广播给所有验证者，消除mempool存在。验证者公开证明接收payload并运行共识决定区块内容，同时通过确定性排序算法对区块内payload进行排序。

Result: 当所有正确验证者对给定payload提供证明时，AMP保证该payload将被包含在下一区块；区块可包含来自多个提议者的payload实现批量最终化。这一有界包含保证结合确定性排序算法，有效遏制了单一验证者的权力。

Conclusion: AMP通过多层提议者架构和分离传播与共识的设计原则，有效限制了单一验证者的权力，在保证交易确定性的同时提升了吞吐量。

Abstract: Blockchain systems that settle financial transactions face a structural tension: the single validator that assembles each block holds unilateral power over transaction inclusion and ordering. Traditional markets curb this very power through front-running and market-manipulation laws. Regulators have flagged the absence of such rules as a first-order concern for blockchain-based financial infrastructure. In response, we introduce AMP, a multi-proposer protocol, on top of the Tendermint consensus algorithm, where no validator can control the flow of transactions into blocks. Instead, dedicated nodes called proposers sit between users and validators. They collect user transactions, group them into payloads, and broadcast the payloads to all validators. Consequently, there is no mempool, and AMP applies the design principle of separating dissemination from agreement, which can lead to higher throughput. Validators publicly attest to receiving payloads and run consensus to decide the set of payloads to include in the next block. When all correct validators attest to a given payload, AMP guarantees that payload will be included in the next block; a block thus contains payloads from multiple proposers, allowing for bulk finalization. This bounded inclusion guarantee along with a deterministic ordering algorithm which is run over all payloads included in a block, curbs the power of any single validator. Validators no longer control what is included in a block, nor can they arbitrarily order the contents of blocks.

</details>


### [171] [Flare: Leveraging Serverless Elasticity to Absorb Microservice Load Spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23707)
*Dilina Dehigama,Shyam Jesalpura,David Schall,Antonios Katsarakis,Marios Kogias,Rakesh Kumar,Boris Grot*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Online services strive to maintain application responsiveness even when the traffic is unpredictable and fluctuating. Today's online services are commonly deployed as chains of microservices, each microservice packaged as one or more containers inside virtual machines (VMs). While performant and affordable when the load is steady, VM-based deployments are known to be slow to scale when the load spikes, resulting in degraded performance for end-users of the service. To avoid such performance degradations, service providers can over-provision their deployments; however, such a strategy is costly and inefficient, leaving resources under-utilized for extended periods.
  To address the challenge of unpredictable load spikes, we propose Flare, a hybrid microservice architecture that combines VMs with serverless computing. Flare utilizes VMs to cost-effectively handle steady workloads and leverages serverless elasticity to absorb traffic spikes. When a spike occurs, Flare detects which specific service(s) are overloaded and shifts the excess load of only those services to serverless, thus minimizing the cost overhead. Flare seamlessly integrates into existing auto-scaling and serverless infrastructure, requiring minimal changes to the control plane and no modifications to the application.

</details>


### [172] [HyperParallel-MoE: Multi-Core Interleaved Scheduling for Fast MoE Training on Ascend NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23764)
*Zewen Jin,Congkun Ai,Guangpeng Zhang,Hanbo Zhang,Haoran Wang,Shihan Xiao,Da Lei,Xuefeng Jin,Teng Su,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对Ascend NPUs上MoE训练效率低的问题，提出了HyperParallel-MoE框架，通过瓦片级异构调度统一利用AIC和AIV资源，实现通信与计算的细粒度Overlap，使MoE-FFN延迟降低1.58倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代MoE模型依赖大规模AI加速器集群训练，Ascend NPUs提供异构计算资源（AIC矩阵单元、AIV向量单元及跨队列同步支持），但现有框架以串行方式执行MoE算子，导致异构并行性未被充分利用。

Method: 提出HyperParallel-MoE编译调度框架：1）将算子级MoE执行转换为静态调度瓦片级异构任务流；2）引入AIV驱动的单边通信消除主机端集合同步；3）依赖保持的瓦片任务生成统一通信与计算；4）事件驱动静态调度协调跨队列执行；5）在统一运行时中单次内核启动并发驱动AIC和AIV worker，实现通信、矩阵计算、向量计算的细粒度Overlap。

Result: 在Ascend A3集群上使用DeepSeek风格MoE模型评估，多专家并行配置下，Dispatch-to-Combine MoE-FFN延迟降低最高1.58倍。

Conclusion: 瓦片级异构调度可显著提升现代NPUs上MoE训练效率，HyperParallel-MoE在Ascend A3集群上验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models increasingly rely on large-scale AI accelerator clusters for efficient training. Ascend NPUs expose heterogeneous on-chip compute resources, including matrix-oriented AIC units and vector-oriented AIV units with explicit cross-queue synchronization support. However, existing training frameworks largely execute MoE operators in a serialized kernel-by-kernel manner, leaving substantial heterogeneous parallelism underutilized.
  This paper presents HyperParallel-MoE, a compilation and scheduling framework for MoE training on Ascend NPUs. HyperParallel-MoE transforms operator-level MoE execution into a statically scheduled tile-level heterogeneous taskflow spanning AIC and AIV resources. It introduces AIV-driven one-sided communication to eliminate host-side collective synchronization, dependency-preserving tile task generation to unify communication and computation under a common task abstraction, and event-driven static scheduling to coordinate cross-queue execution with low runtime overhead. HyperParallel-MoE further executes the compiled taskflow within a unified runtime that concurrently drives AIC and AIV workers inside a single kernel launch, enabling fine-grained overlap among communication, matrix computation, and vector computation while preserving existing optimized operators. We implement HyperParallel-MoE in the MindSpore and MindFormers stack and evaluate it using DeepSeek-style MoE models on Ascend A3 clusters. Across multiple expert-parallel configurations, HyperParallel-MoE reduces Dispatch-to-Combine MoE-FFN latency by up to 1.58x, demonstrating that tile-level heterogeneous scheduling can substantially improve MoE training efficiency on modern NPUs.

</details>


### [173] [Enhancing Energy Efficiency in Scientific Workflows through CFD based PIVAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23850)
*Ali Zahir,Ashiq Anjum,Mark Wilkinson,Jeyan Thiyagalingam*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于CFD与物理信息变分自编码器(PIVAE)的AI辅助HPC调度框架，通过生成物理真实工作负载数据优化能耗管理。研究表明CPU性能降低15%可实现10%节能，仅增加5-6%周转时间，为下一代HPC基础设施的可持续发展调度提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC环境中科学工作流的规模与复杂度增长导致能耗管理面临挑战，传统调度策略未能充分考虑热力学行为与系统可扩展性之间的复杂相互作用。

Method: 集成计算流体力学(CFD)与物理信息变分自编码器(PIVAE)，生成物理真实的合成工作负载数据；基于资源利用率特征对工作流进行分类，评估局部感知调度和投机感知调度等策略。

Result: 将CPU性能降低至15%可节省约10%能源，同时仅增加5-6%的周转时间，找到了最优运行甜点区间。

Conclusion: Physics-informed generative modeling enables adaptive, sustainable, and data-efficient scheduling for next-generation HPC infrastructures.

Abstract: The growing complexity and scale of scientific workflows in high performance computing (HPC) environments have led to significant challenges in managing energy consumption without compromising computational performance. Traditional scheduling strategies often fail to account for the complex interplay between thermal dynamics, workload diversity, and system scalability, leading to inefficient and unsustainable energy usage. This paper introduces a novel, scalable, and AI-assisted scheduling framework for optimizing energy consumption in HPC environments without compromising performance. Central to our approach is the integration of Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) with a Physics-Informed Variational Autoencoder (PIVAE), enabling the generation of physically realistic synthetic workload data that bridges the gap between thermodynamic behavior and scheduler decision-making in complex, multi-scale HPC environments. By categorizing workflows based on resource utilization profiles, we evaluate multiple scheduling strategies such as Locality Aware and Speculative Aware Scheduling. These workflows, ranging from event reconstruction to anomaly detection, represent diverse computational intensities. Our results show that modest reductions in CPU performance (e.g., to 15%) can yield substantial energy savings (up to 10%) with only minor turnaround time increases (approximately 5-6%), identifying an optimal operational sweet spot. This work demonstrates how physics-informed generative modeling can enable adaptive, sustainable, and data-efficient scheduling for next-generation HPC infrastructures.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

### [174] [LLM Code Smells: A Taxonomy and Detection Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22976)
*Zacharie Chenail-Larcher,Brahim Mahmoudi,Naouel Moha,Quentin Stiévenart,Florent Avellaneda*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: The paper addresses quality issues from poor LLM integration in software systems by establishing a taxonomy and catalog of nine LLM code smells, and developing a static analysis tool (SpecDetect4LLM) that effectively detects these smells across large-scale open-source projects.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Poor LLM integration in source code can undermine software system quality; inadequate coding practices need documentation to help developers mitigate issues.

Method: Proposed a taxonomy and catalog of nine LLM code smells; developed SpecDetect4LLM static analysis tool; conducted empirical evaluation on 692 open-source projects (171,194 source files).

Result: LLM code smells are prevalent in 73.5% of systems; detection tool achieves high precision (91.3%) and acceptable recall (71.8%).

Conclusion: LLM code smells affect 73.5% of analyzed systems; SpecDetect4LLM achieves 91.3% precision and 71.8% recall in detection.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into software systems for diverse purposes, due to their versatility, flexibility, and ability to simulate human reasoning to some extent. However, poor integration of LLM inference in source code can undermine software system quality. Therefore, inadequate LLM integration coding practices must be documented to help developers mitigate such issues. Following our earlier work on LLM code smells, this paper consolidates and refines the concept by presenting a self-contained taxonomy and a catalog of nine LLM code smells. We also create SpecDetect4LLM, a static source code analysis tool for their detection, and conduct extensive empirical evaluations of its detection effectiveness (precision and recall) as well as the prevalence of LLM code smells across 692 open-source software projects (171,194 source files). Our results show that LLM code smells affect 73.5% of the analyzed systems, with a detection precision of 91.3% and a recall of 71.8%.

</details>


### [175] [Finding Performance Issues in Database Systems by Exploiting Dormant Code Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22992)
*Jinsheng Ba,Zhendong Su*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: This paper proposes Branch Flip Analysis (BFA), a white-box methodology for systematically uncovering performance issues in DBMSs by flipping code branches to test whether optimizations actually improve performance. Implemented in QueryZen and validated on PostgreSQL, MySQL, CockroachDB, and MariaDB, resulting in 21 previously unknown issues.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Performance issues in DBMSs are difficult to detect because no ground-truth performance baseline exists. Existing black-box methods cannot systematically test individual optimizations, leaving many performance issues undiscovered despite decades of optimization work.

Method: Branch Flip Analysis (BFA) flips code branches to enforce or disable specific optimizations, then compares performance differences. If disabling an optimization does not yield significantly worse performance, it indicates a performance issue. Implemented in prototype system QueryZen.

Result: Found 21 previously unknown and unique performance issues across four mature DBMSs using industry-standard benchmarks.

Conclusion: QueryZen successfully identified 21 previously unknown and unique performance issues across four major DBMSs (PostgreSQL, MySQL, CockroachDB, MariaDB) using TPC-H and TPC-DS benchmarks. BFA's core concept is simple, broadly applicable, and can be adapted to other software systems for performance analysis.

Abstract: Performance is a critical characteristic of fundamental systems, such as Database Management Systems (DBMSs). Both academia and industry have invested decades in exploring efficient optimization algorithms. Despite these efforts, DBMSs are prone to performance issues, which incur suboptimal performance. Finding such issues is a longstanding challenge as no ground-truth performance is available. Existing work adopts black-box methods to examine performance consistency across executions, but cannot systematically test optimizations. In this work, we propose a novel, general white-box methodology, Branch Flip Analysis (BFA), to systematically and effectively uncover performance issues. BFA flips code branches to enforce or disable an optimization, and the performance is expected to be not significantly better. Otherwise, a performance issue exists. BFA provides a new perspective to finding performance issues and testing optimization logics in a fine-grained manner. We realized BFA in a prototype system QueryZen, and evaluated it on four widely-used and mature DBMSs: PostgreSQL, MySQL, CockroachDB, and MariaDB. QueryZen found 21 previously unknown and unique performance issues with the workload of the extensively used benchmarks TPC-H and TPC-DS. The core concept of BFA is simple and broadly applicable, and can be adapted to analyze the performance of other software systems.

</details>


### [176] [A measurement substrate for agentic Kubernetes operations: Methodology and a case study in retrieval-compounding falsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23058)
*Joshua Odmark,Gideon Rubin,Deon van der Vyver*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究指出当前运维智能体实证研究缺乏可证伪性，提出了agent-breakage闭环测量框架，通过故障注入和四维评分系统解决验证基础设施缺失问题。框架成功捕获了三个会误导研究结论的混杂因素，检索增强实验显示效果微弱且不稳定，提示该方向需更审慎评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运维智能体的实证研究存在根本性缺陷：缺乏对照基线比较、选择偏差普遍存在、缺少预注册决策矩阵、样本量不足以应对评分系统的噪声水平。其根本原因在于运维领域缺少类似代码智能体的快速可证伪的验证基础设施。

Method: agent-breakage是一个闭环测量框架，通过向目标Kubernetes集群注入故障、观察自主智能体的响应、按四个维度评分、积累(状态,动作,结果)标注元组来评估智能体表现。框架创新点包括：1)通过确定性嵌入器实现真正的关闭对照；2)区分框架错误与推理错误；3)强制执行预注册决策矩阵。在案例研究中进行了360次运行的语料密度扫描实验。

Result: 该框架在案例研究中识别出三个混杂因素：pgvector索引Bug导致+19%的选择偏差 artifact，小样本高估效应约3倍。检索增强实验结果：3个密集语料场景中仅1个达到p<0.05显著性，合并效应+3.9个百分点，在n=60样本下不显著。360次运行显示近邻对齐机制比原始计数更主导性能。

Conclusion: agent-breakage框架为运维智能体提供了等效于代码智能体的验证基础设施，可有效区分框架错误与推理错误，强制预注册决策矩阵。该研究揭示了检索增强型运维智能体受限于样本量不足和选择偏差，当前证据尚不支持其实际部署价值。

Abstract: Empirical claims about autonomous Kubernetes operations agents are largely unfalsifiable. Published work reports observational results without controlled comparisons against an agent-disabled baseline, selection bias is endemic, pre-registered decision matrices are absent, and samples are typically too small for the noise level of the underlying scoring system. The cause is the same gap that limits the agents themselves: code agents have a verification substrate that turns "did it work" into a fast, falsifiable, ground-truth signal, and operations has nothing equivalent. We present agent-breakage, a closed-loop measurement framework that injects faults into a target Kubernetes cluster, observes how an autonomous agent responds, scores the response on four axes against ground truth, and accumulates outcome-labeled (state, action, outcome) tuples. The framework distinguishes framework error from reasoning error, supports a true off-condition control via a deterministic-embedder mechanism, and enforces pre-registered decision matrices. We use it as a case study to test whether retrieval over past postmortems compounds an agent's capability. The methodological payload is three confounds the substrate caught during that case study, each of which would have produced a wrong published claim on a less instrumented version of the same work: a pgvector index bug, a +19% selection-bias artifact, and small-sample estimates that overstated effects by roughly 3x. The retrieval result itself is a partial falsification: 1 of 3 dense-corpus scenarios significant at p<0.05, pooled effect +3.9 percentage points, not significant at n=60. A within-scenario corpus-density sweep at 360 runs shows that mechanistic alignment of near-neighbors dominates raw count. The framework is released open source.

</details>


### [177] [Security of LLM-generated Code: A Comparative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23091)
*Srivathsan G Morkonda,Mahmoud Selim,Hala Assal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 七个大语言模型（LLM）均生成含有安全漏洞的代码，且大多数漏洞属于严重或高危级别，AI辅助代码生成存在显著安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Growing adoption of AI tools by software developers, driven by productivity and learning benefits, raises concerns about associated risks, particularly software security vulnerabilities in LLM-generated code.

Method: Empirical evaluation of code generated by seven popular LLMs, building upon previous work to mimic developer behavior when using LLMs for code generation.

Result: All seven evaluated LLMs generate code containing vulnerabilities, with the majority being of critical or high severity.

Conclusion: All seven evaluated LLMs generate code containing vulnerabilities, presenting significant security risks when used for code generation in development processes.

Abstract: The majority of software developers use or are planning to use Artificial Intelligence (AI) tools in their development processes. Their top reasons include improving productivity and faster learning. In fact, Large Language Model (LLM)-generated code is currently in production, including in major tech companies. However, concerns were raised about the risks associated with the use of AI tools to generate code. In this paper, we focus our attention on the risks to software security. We empirically evaluate the security of code generated by seven popular LLMs. We build upon previous work to mimic the behaviours of developers when using LLMs to generate code. Our results show that all seven LLMs that we have evaluated generate code that contains vulnerabilities, the majority of which are of critical or high severity.

</details>


### [178] [Philosophical Dispositions as Behavioral Constraints for AI-Assisted Code Review: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23108)
*Kaushal Bansal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过将哲学认识论传统（怀疑主义、尼耶也逻辑、犬儒主义、儒家伦理）构建为AI代码审查的"人格透镜"，提出了一种多取向约束系统。该系统在保持模型分析视角的同时，实现46%人类一致性、75%独特发现率、零误报，且51%的发现优于通用提示词基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current AI-assisted code review tools operate as generic "expert reviewers" that produce homogeneous findings regardless of the specific analysis type needed, lacking diversity and targeted focus on structurally different issue types.

Method: Proposed a multi-disposition AI code review system grounded in philosophical traditions (Pyrrhonist Skepticism, Navya-Nyāya logic, Diogenes' Cynicism, Confucian relational ethics). Each disposition is defined apophatically (by refusal rules) with embedded hamartia (self-monitoring failure modes), orchestrated through role protocols. Evaluation on 50 merged PRs across 7 repos, 5 languages, 5 orgs compared against human reviewers and generic "expert reviewer" baseline.

Result: Achieved 46% convergence with human reviewers, 75% unique findings rate, and 0 false positives across 601 findings. Baseline comparison shows 51% of disposition findings are not produced by generic prompting. Cross-model validation demonstrates 100% framework-structure adherence with 39% finding-level agreement between Claude Opus and GPT Codex 5.3-xhigh.

Conclusion: The philosophical disposition framework provides meaningful behavioral constraints on AI code reviewers while preserving model-specific analytical perspectives. The approach offers a viable path toward more diverse, targeted, and epistemologically-grounded AI-assisted code review.

Abstract: AI-assisted code review tools typically operate as generic "expert reviewer" agents, producing homogeneous findings regardless of the analysis type needed. We present a system that constrains AI reviewer behavior through philosophical dispositions -- coherent personality lenses grounded in specific epistemological traditions (Pyrrhonist Skepticism, Navya-Ny=aya logic, Diogenes' Cynicism, Confucian relational ethics) that direct attention to structurally different types of issues. Each disposition is defined apophatically (by what it refuses to do), equipped with a self-monitoring failure mode (hamartia), and orchestrated in sequence by role protocols.
  We evaluate this system on 50 merged pull requests across 7 repositories spanning 5 programming languages (Python, Go, C++, Java, Terraform), 5 organizations (2 enterprise, 3 open-source), and 2 temporal eras (pre-AI 2020, post-AI 2024--2026). The disposition system achieves 46% convergence with human reviewers (validating signal quality), identifies unique findings at a 75% rate, and produces no findings judged false-positive by the author across 601 total findings (inter-rater agreement was not assessed and remains a limitation). A controlled baseline comparison demonstrates that 51% of disposition findings are not produced by the same model using generic "expert reviewer" prompting, and these unique findings target structural, operational, and logical concerns rather than standard code-level issues. Preliminary cross-model validation (Claude Opus vs.\ GPT Codex 5.3-xhigh) on 3 PRs shows 100% framework-structure adherence with 39% finding-level agreement, suggesting the framework provides real behavioral constraint while preserving model-specific analytical perspective.

</details>


### [179] [The Impact of AI Coding Assistants on Software Engineering: A Longitudinal Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23135)
*Annie Vella,Kelly Blincoe*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这项纵向研究表明，AI编码助手显著改变了软件开发工作模式：代码编写时间大幅减少，工作重心转向验证和监督AI输出。虽然大多数工程师认为生产力有所提升，但开发者体验却在多个关键维度上恶化，揭示了生产力指标与实际工作体验之间的显著矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编码助手的普及，需要系统性地理解这些工具如何影响专业软件开发者的日常工作体验、生产力和任务焦点。

Method: 纵向混合方法研究。通过两次问卷调查（间隔六个月），收集了158名首次参与者、101名第二次参与者和95名匹配纵向队列的数据，对专业软件工程师进行跟踪调查。

Result: 1. 大多数开发任务的投入时间减少，82%报告编写代码时间减少；2. 工作焦点从创建活动向验证活动转移；3. 提出"监督工程工作"新类别；4. 生产力-体验悖论：生产力感知稳定（84%报告提升），但匹配队列中报告开发者体验恶化比例从14%翻倍至27%；5. 心流状态和认知负荷恶化，但反馈循环改善。

Conclusion: AI coding assistants正在同时影响软件工程工作的本质和工程师的工作体验。研究揭示了生产力-体验悖论：尽管生产力感知保持稳定，但开发者体验在多个维度上出现恶化，尤其是心流状态和认知负荷。研究强调了"监督工程工作"这一新工作类别的出现，并建议组织需要重新思考绩效评估和开发者体验优化策略。

Abstract: AI coding assistants have become prolific in recent years. Through a longitudinal mixed-methods investigation, we examined how professional software engineers perceive the effects of AI coding assistants in regard to task focus, developer experience, and productivity. Two questionnaires were administered six months apart, yielding 158 eligible participants at the first time point, 101 at the second, and a matched longitudinal cohort of 95. Participants reported spending less time on most development tasks, with 82% reporting less on writing code. We find broader shift in focus from creation to verification activities. We propose a new category of work we term supervisory engineering work, encompassing the direction, evaluation, and correction of AI output. We also identified a productivity-experience paradox: productivity perceptions held stable, with 84% reporting improvement at both time points, yet among matched participants, the proportion reporting worsened developer experience in at least one dimension nearly doubled from 14% to 27%, with flow state and cognitive load eroding while feedback loops improved. These findings suggest that AI coding assistants are impacting both the nature of software engineering work and how engineers experience it.

</details>


### [180] [MISRust: Mapping MISRA-C++ Coding Guidelines to the Rust Programming Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23490)
*Marius Molz,Niels Schneider,Sven Lechner,Stefan Kowalewski,Alexandru Kampmann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统地将MISRA C++ 2023的179条编码规范映射到Rust语言，发现47.75%的适用规则被Rust语言设计自动强制执行，69条规则仍需直接应用或适配，36条规则仅在使用safe Rust时自动满足。提出了Rust特定规范的适配方案，并识别了需要新建Rust专用规范的领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rust语言在安全关键系统开发中受到关注，但ISO 26262等安全标准要求的MISRA C++编码规范尚不存在于Rust生态中，需要系统性地建立Rust编码指南以满足安全关键系统的合规性要求。

Method: 逐条分析179条MISRA C++ 2023编码规范的制定理由，基于其意图判断在Rust编程语境中的适用性，并将所有规则分为6个类别。同时明确区分safe Rust和unsafe Rust的使用场景。

Result: 111条规则被认定为直接适用，其中47.75%（约53条）被Rust语言设计自动强制执行；69条规则仍需直接应用或适配；36条规则仅在使用safe Rust子集时自动满足，但在使用unsafe Rust时需重新强制执行。

Conclusion: MISRA规范中约61%可映射到Rust，其中约一半被语言设计自动覆盖；safe/unsafe Rust的区分对规范适用性有重要影响；识别了需要新建Rust专用规范的特定领域；提供了开源的映射结果和工具支持。

Abstract: The Rust programming language is increasingly being considered for safety-critical system development. However, established safety standards such as ISO 26262 require the use of coding guidelines that do not yet exist for Rust.
