On GPT-5.5, it improves average no-skill accuracy by +23.5 points in direct chat, +24.8 in Codex agentic loop, and +19.1 in Claude Code. Optimized skills also transfer across model scales, execution environments, and to related benchmarks.

Conclusion: SkillOpt is the first systematic controllable text-space optimizer for agent skills. It establishes a new paradigm where skills are trained with the same reproducibility discipline as weight-space optimization, achieving state-of-the-art results across 52 evaluated cells. The approach demonstrates strong transferability, with optimized skills retaining value across model scales, execution environments, and related benchmarks. This work opens up a new research direction for systematic skill optimization in LLM agents.

Abstract: Agent skills today are hand-crafted, generated one-shot, or evolved through loosely controlled self-revision, none of which behaves like a deep-learning optimizer for the skill, and none of which reliably improves over its starting point under feedback. We argue the skill should instead be trained as the external state of a frozen agent, with the same discipline that makes weight-space optimization reproducible. SkillOpt is, to our knowledge, the first systematic controllable text-space optimizer for agent skills: a separate optimizer model turns scored rollouts into bounded add/delete/replace edits on a single skill document, and an edit is accepted only when it strictly improves a held-out validation score. A textual learning-rate budget, rejected-edit buffer, and epoch-wise slow/meta update make skill training stable while adding zero inference-time model calls at deployment. Across six benchmarks, seven target models, and three execution harnesses (direct chat, Codex, Claude Code), SkillOpt is best or tied on all 52 evaluated (model, benchmark, harness) cells and beats every per-cell competitor among human, one-shot LLM, Trace2Skill, TextGrad, GEPA, and EvoSkill skills. On GPT-5.5 it lifts the average no-skill accuracy by +23.5 points in direct chat, by +24.8 inside the Codex agentic loop, and by +19.1 inside Claude Code. Transfer experiments further show that optimized skill artifacts retain value when moved across model scales, between Codex and Claude Code execution environments, and to a nearby math benchmark without further optimization.

</details>

### [212] [Whose Good, Whose Place? The Moral Geography of Agentic AI for Social Good](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22995)
*Poli Nemkova,Haeshitha Indukuri,Jaedon Charles*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对2015-2026年间发表的112篇关于社会公益智能体AI的论文进行了系统调查，发现存在"道德-地理不对称"现象：论文在最需要本地政治、法律和文化背景的领域（如制度性目标SDG 16）反而最不明确地理背景（仅13%），而健康或生态领域则有37-40%。73%的论文未指定任何地理背景，75%未报告任何真实世界部署或小规模测试。作者识别出五个问责缺口并提出最低报告标准建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体AI系统常被宣称用于社会公益并与联合国可持续发展目标(SDGs)挂钩，但这些社会公益声明并未建立对目标社区的问责机制，亟需系统评估现有研究的地理特异性与实践落地情况。

Method: 对112篇智能体AI社会公益论文进行结构化调查分析，按SDGs类别进行分类统计，重点考察地理背景说明率和真实世界部署情况。

Result: 发现严重的道德-地理不对称：73%论文无地理背景说明；健康/生态相关SDGs论文37-40%说明地理背景，而制度/社会政策SDGs仅13%；SDG 16（和平、正义与强大机构）覆盖最多但地理说明率最低；仅25%论文报告真实世界部署或小规模测试。

Conclusion: 智能体AI社会公益研究普遍存在"道德抽象化"倾向，将制度性公益视为普适真理而非需要情境化理解的事项。作者识别出五个 accountability gaps（问责缺口），并提出针对智能体AI社会公益应用的最低报告标准框架，以促进更具情境特异性、参与性和问责性的研究实践。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are increasingly proposed for social-good domains, often invoking the United Nations Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs) as a vocabulary of global benefit. Yet claims of social good do not establish accountability to the communities a system claims to serve. We present a structured survey of 112 papers on agentic AI for social good published between 2015 and 2026.
  We find a moral-geographic asymmetry: papers are least likely to specify geographic context in precisely the domains where local political, legal, and cultural context matters most. Across the corpus, 82 of 112 papers (73%) specify no geographic context. Papers aligned with health or physical/ecological SDGs specify geography 37-40% of the time, while papers aligned with institutional and social-policy SDGs do so only 13%. SDG 16, peace, justice, and strong institutions, is both the most-covered goal in the corpus and the one with the lowest geographic-specification rate.
  We interpret this as moral abstraction: agentic AI for social good often treats institutional good as universal in ways it does not treat health or ecological good. A second finding compounds this: only 28 of 112 papers (25%) report any real-world deployment or small-scale test. We identify five accountability gaps and propose a minimal reporting standard for more context-specific, participatory, and accountable agentic AI for social good.

</details>


### [213] [Opportunities and Risks of Generative AI through the Health Information Journey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23026)
*Matthew R. DeVerna,Harry Yaojun Yan,Kai-Cheng Yang,Filippo Menczer*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: This paper introduces a conceptual framework examining how AI transforms public engagement with health information and formal healthcare, identifying both opportunities (access, comprehension, care continuity) and risks (inaccurate content, manipulation, opaque automated decisions).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI systems are increasingly integrated into both health information consumption and clinical decision-making, creating dual opportunities for improved access/comprehension and risks of unreliable or opaque content.

Method: Conceptual framework development; proposes a four-stage model categorizing AI applications as the public transitions from information environments to clinical care settings.

Result: N/A - This is a conceptual/framework paper introducing a research agenda rather than reporting empirical findings.

Conclusion: A four-stage framework is proposed to map opportunities and risks across the continuum from information seeking to formal healthcare encounters.

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is fundamentally changing how health content is encountered and acted upon across both the information and healthcare ecosystems. AI systems now generate claims, curate information, interpret symptoms, synthesize evidence, and guide decisions, with significant opportunities and risks for the public. Potential benefits include improvements in access, comprehension, and continuity of care. At the same time, AI can introduce inaccurate or manipulative content that is difficult to distinguish from reliable guidance, and encourage automated decisions that affect care with little transparency or recourse. We introduce a four-stage framework to examine how these opportunities and risks unfold as the public moves through the information environment and into formal healthcare.

</details>


### [214] [Defining AI Fatigue in Academic Contexts: Dimensions, Indicators, and a Stage-Based Model Using Grounded Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23123)
*John Paul P. Miranda,Emmanuel B. Parreño,Jovita G. Rivera*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究针对学术场景中AI工具使用引发的独特疲劳现象，基于菲律宾三所高校1,054名学生的质性数据，通过扎根理论识别出AI疲劳的五维结构（认知、动机、道德、身体、注意力），并构建了AI Fatigue Model来解释各压力源如何随反复交互而累积与相互强化，为该构念的进一步量化验证和应用奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论框架（如技术压力technostress、数字疲劳digital fatigue）尚未充分解释AI工具在学术场景中引发的新型疲劳形式，亟需建立针对AI使用情境的独特构念。

Method: 基于1,054名菲律宾三所高校学生的开放式回答，采用扎根理论分析法（grounded theory analysis）构建概念模型，从认知、动机、情绪、身体和注意力五个维度分析学生在AI辅助学术工作中的压力体验。

Result: 识别出AI疲劳的五个核心维度：认知超载（Cognitive Overload）、动机脱离（Motivational Disengagement）、道德焦虑（Moral Unease）、身体疲劳（Physical Strain）和注意力分散（Attentional Drift），每个维度包含两个基于参与者描述的指标。

Conclusion: 研究建立了AI疲劳作为独立构念的概念基础，并提出AI Fatigue Model（阶段式框架），为未来量表开发、跨情境验证及AI辅助学习环境中的学生心理健康研究奠定基础。

Abstract: The integration of AI tools in academic settings has introduced a distinct form of strain that existing frameworks like technostress and digital fatigue have not yet fully addressed. This study develops a conceptual model and identifies the dimensions that define AI fatigue as a form of strain arising from sustained academic use of AI tools. Using grounded theory analysis of open-ended responses from 1,054 university students across three universities in the Philippines, the study examined the cognitive, motivational, emotional, physical, and attentional pressures students experienced during AI-supported academic work. Analysis produced five dimensions of AI fatigue, namely Cognitive Overload, Motivational Disengagement, Moral Unease, Physical Strain, and Attentional Drift, each consisting of two indicators grounded in participant accounts. The findings also yielded the AI Fatigue Model, a stage-based framework that explains how these pressures accumulate and reinforce one another across repeated AI interaction in academic tasks. These contributions establish a conceptual and exploratory foundation for AI fatigue as a distinct construct and provide a basis for future instrument validation, scale development, and cross-contextual inquiry in academic settings where AI now mediates student learning.

</details>


### [215] [Cognitive offloading and the speedup illusion in human-AI interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23177)
*Sunny Yu,Myra Cheng,Ahmad Jabbar,Ilia Sucholutsky,Katherine M. Collins,Dan Jurafsky,Robert D. Hawkins*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究发现用户系统性地低估AI辅助任务的完成时间，存在"加速幻觉"偏差，但对人类辅助无此偏差；同时主观努力与客观时间分离，提示效率评估需考虑时间与感知的双重维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM有潜力提升人类生产力，但前提是用户能够准确判断何时应将认知工作卸载给AI。现有研究缺乏对用户在估计AI潜在时间节省方面校准程度（calibration）的系统分析。

Method: 一项预先注册的大规模行为研究（N=1237），聚焦简单认知任务，对比独立完成、AI辅助完成与人类辅助三种条件下的预期时间与实际完成时间。

Result: 发现"加速幻觉"现象：实际完成时间在独立与AI辅助条件下无显著差异，但用户预测AI将显著更快；该偏差在想象另一个人类帮助时未出现。此外，时间与努力感知分离：用户报告AI辅助时主观努力更低，尽管实际完成时间相当。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了用户对AI辅助效率的认知偏差——"加速幻觉"，即用户系统性地低估AI辅助任务的完成时间，同时将主观努力与实际时间分离。这表明仅用完成时间来衡量效率增益是不充分的，需要考虑用户感知的多维性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have the potential to boost human productivity by speeding up task completion -- provided users know when to offload cognitive work to them. But we do not know if users are well-calibrated in estimating these potential time savings. We conducted a preregistered large-scale behavioral study (N = 1237) to characterize mismatches between expectations and reality, with a focus on simple cognitive tasks. While actual completion times between independent completion and AI-assisted completion did not differ, participants predicted AI to be significantly faster. The same bias was not observed when imagining help from another human participant. We identify a speedup illusion where people have accurate forecasts of independent completion times but significantly underestimate AI-assisted times. Additionally, time and effort dissociate: participants reported lower subjective effort with AI despite equivalent completion times. This suggests that completion time itself is not sufficient to characterize efficiency gains.

</details>


### [216] [IyàwóBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Model Clinical Triage Accuracy on Undifferentiated Febrile Illness in Nigerian Primary Health Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23465)
*Anthonio Oladimeji Gabriel,Dimeji Abdulsobur Olawuyi,Oloruntoba Ajayi,Temiloluwa Aderemi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究引入IyàwóBench v1.0评估工具，用于测试大型语言模型在尼日利亚初级卫生环境中的临床分诊能力。研究发现LLMs在分诊安全性上表现良好（100%安全率），但分诊准确性差异显著（39.0%-67.5%），集成WHO指南的临床工程系统优于通用模型约28.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Undifferentiated febrile illness is the leading cause of primary care outpatient visits in Nigeria, yet no validated benchmark exists for evaluating LLM clinical triage reasoning in West African primary health settings.

Method: Six LLMs were evaluated on structured triage classification across two metrics: triage accuracy and safety score, using 200 synthetic clinical vignettes derived from statistical distributions of 1,200 real patient encounters at 19 PHCs in Oyo State, Nigeria.

Result: All six models achieved 100% safety scores (never downgrading critical cases), but triage accuracy varied substantially (39.0%-67.5%), with two models showing near-zero accuracy due to structured output non-compliance. Clinically engineered systems outperformed general-purpose models by up to 28.5 percentage points.

Conclusion: IyàwóBench provides the first reproducible evaluation framework for LLM clinical decision support in West African primary care. Modern LLMs exhibit safe triage behavior but vary substantially in structured clinical accuracy. Clinically engineered systems with embedded WHO guidelines outperform general-purpose models by up to 28.5 percentage points.

Abstract: Background. Undifferentiated febrile illness is the leading cause of primary care outpatient visits in Nigeria, yet no validated benchmark exists for evaluating large language model (LLM) clinical triage reasoning in West African primary health settings. Methods. We introduce IyàwóBench v1.0, a dataset of 200 synthetic clinical vignettes across eight febrile illness categories derived from statistical distributions of 1,200 real patient encounters at 19 primary health centres (PHCs) in Oyo State, Nigeria. Six LLMs were evaluated on structured triage classification across two metrics: triage accuracy and safety score. Results. All six models achieved 100% safety scores (95% CI: 96.4-100.0%), never downgrading a critical REFER NOW case to TREAT HERE. Triage accuracy varied substantially: Claude Sonnet (claude-sonnet-4-5) 67.5% (95% CI: 60.8-73.7%), Llama 4 Scout 59.5% (52.5-66.2%), Llama 3.3 70B 43.0% (36.2-50.0%), and Llama 3.1 8B 39.0% (32.4-45.9%). Two models demonstrated near-zero accuracy attributable to structured output non-compliance. Conclusions. Modern LLMs exhibit safe triage behaviour but vary substantially in structured clinical accuracy. Clinically engineered systems with embedded WHO guidelines outperform general-purpose models by up to 28.5 percentage points. IyàwóBench provides the first reproducible evaluation framework for LLM clinical decision support in West African primary care.

</details>


### [217] [Strategic Stalemates: The Paradox of Export Controls in the U.S.-China AI Race](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23475)
*Jingwen Liu,Jyh-An Lee*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文分析了美国对华AI领域出口管制的战略有效性及法律合规性，认为虽然出口管制是美国遏制中国AI发展的战略工具，但长期效果存疑且可能适得其反，并进一步从WTO/GATT视角论证商业或军民两用AI产品不符合国家安全例外的法律标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在中美AI竞争背景下，美国出口管制政策的战略有效性存疑，且可能产生反效果；同时，过度管制面临WTO法律挑战，需要明确安全例外条款的适用边界。

Method: 通过追踪2022年10月以来的政策演变，对比中美双方措施，从法律层面分析WTO合规性，并结合GATT条款评估出口管制的正当性。

Result: 研究表明：（1）长期看，出口管制可能无意中增强中国的自主研发能力；（2）过于严格或随意的管制可能违反WTO义务，阻碍AI发展。核心法律结论：商业或军民两用AI产品不符合GATT第XXI(b)条的安全例外标准。

Conclusion: 过度使用出口管制可能违反WTO义务，应克制对国家安全例外的解释，商业或军民两用AI模型和半导体不符合GATT第XXI(b)条的安全例外标准。

Abstract: Export control is a policy and legal tool to protect national interests by regulating exports of sensitive goods and technology to foreign nations. It has become central to U.S.-China tech rivalry, especially in AI. Controls cover advanced chips, capital, personnel, and critical minerals for semiconductors. Since October 2022, the U.S. BIS has progressively tightened restrictions on advanced computing components to China. China responded with export curbs on critical minerals and filed a WTO complaint against the U.S. under GATT. This article argues that while export controls are strategic in U.S.-China AI competition, their long-term effectiveness is questionable. They often unintentionally boost China's self-reliance and R&D. Moreover, overly strict or arbitrary controls may violate WTO obligations, complicating dispute resolution and hindering AI progress. The study further examines legal implications of overusing export controls. It advocate for a restrained interpretation of security interests, arguing that commercial or dual-use AI models and semiconductors do not meet the security exception criteria under GATT Article XXI(b).

</details>


### [218] [Unjust Enrichment as a Remedy for AI's Unauthorised Use of Protected Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23503)
*Yangzi Li,Jyh-An Lee*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: The paper proposes unjust enrichment as a superior alternative legal doctrine for resolving disputes arising from unauthorised use of data in generative AI training, arguing it shifts focus from wrongful conduct to recovering unjustly obtained benefits.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Unauthorised use of data in generative AI training creates legal disputes that current IP and privacy frameworks inadequately resolve.

Method: Analyzes unjust enrichment as an alternative legal doctrine to IP infringement and privacy violations, examining gain-based restitution remedies.

Result: Unjust enrichment can capture the wrongfulness of unauthorised data use and may offer more advantageous remedies than existing legal, equitable, and statutory options.

Conclusion: Unjust enrichment offers a pragmatic and equitable framework that reconciles data owners' rights with AI developers' interests.

Abstract: The unauthorised use of data in the training of generative AI models presents significant legal challenges, particularly under intellectual property (IP) and privacy laws. These frameworks frequently grapple with the intricate relationship between data ownership and AI innovation, resulting in ongoing debates regarding optimal protection and enforceability. This article delves into considerable potential of unjust enrichment as an alternative legal doctrine for resolving disputes arising from such unauthorised data use.
  We explore how the concept of unjust enrichment captures the wrongfulness of unauthorised data use in a manner distinct from IP infringement and privacy violations. Furthermore, we analyse the extent to which gain-based restitution for unjust enrichment may prove more advantageous than existing remedies, including legal, equitable, and statutory options. We content that by shifting the emphasis from establishing wrongful conduct to recovering benefits obtained unjustly, unjust enrichment offers a pragmatic and equitable framework that reconciles the rights of data owners with the interests of AI developers.

</details>


### [219] [Engagement-Optimized Care: When LLMs become Mental Health Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23787)
*Briana Vecchione,Meryl Ye,Livia Garofalo,Ranjit Singh*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了LLM作为心理健康基础设施的结构性不公平问题：用户因缺乏替代方案被迫接受风险，而系统设计优化的是参与度而非关怀 Accountability。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM被广泛用作心理健康支持工具，但这些系统既未按护理技术标准设计，也缺乏相应治理机制，其主导设计激励机制以用户参与度为核心优化目标，与用户福祉之间存在根本矛盾。

Method: 采用定性纵向研究设计，18名美国参与者经历了多个研究阶段：初始访谈、四周期日记研究、焦点小组和退出访谈，追踪其LLM使用轨迹。

Result: 研究发现参与者因其他支持形式不可用、负担不起、社会成本过高或不足而转向LLM；设计特征（拟人化提示、默认验证、持续响应、弱脱离机制）塑造了持续依赖；参与者同时体验到有意义的支持与依赖、认知扭曲、隐私期望与法律保护缺失、风险认知下的持续使用。

Conclusion: 该研究呼吁将AI责任追溯至系统成为护理基础设施的设计层和激励机制，而非仅停留在输出层面或危机应对层。具体而言，需要重新设计催生依赖性的系统特征，并建立基于关怀问责的治理框架。

Abstract: General-purpose LLMs are increasingly functioning as mental health infrastructure due to gaps in care left by provider shortages, inadequate insurance coverage, social isolation, and stigma around formal help-seeking. This shift poses a distinct problem for AI ethics: systems neither designed nor governed as care technologies are being used as such, while their dominant design incentives optimize for engagement rather than user well-being. We present findings from a qualitative, longitudinal study with 18 US-based participants who use general-purpose LLMs for socioemotional support and participated in one or more of our study phases, including initial interviews, a four-week diary study, focus groups, and exit interviews. Participants turned to LLMs because other forms of support were unavailable, unaffordable, socially costly, or inadequate. As they continued to use these systems, design features such as anthropomorphic cues, default validation, persistent responsiveness, and weak disengagement mechanisms shaped their ongoing reliance. Participants described meaningful support alongside dependency, epistemic distortion through one-sided validation, privacy expectations without corresponding legal protection, and continued use despite awareness of these risks. We argue these dynamics reflect a structurally unfair tradeoff: users accept risks because support is otherwise absent, while available systems are optimized to deepen engagement and lack care-based accountability. The paper makes three contributions: it traces the arc through which LLMs become care infrastructure and identifies distinct ethical tensions at each stage, shifts analysis from turn-based exchanges to longitudinal trajectories of use, and argues that accountability belongs at the design and incentive conditions through which these systems become care infrastructure rather than at the output or crisis-response layer.

</details>


### [220] [Inferential Privacy Leakage in Anonymized Conversational AI Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23820)
*S M Mehedi Zaman,Kiran Garimella*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究对全球南部四国1000余名用户的ChatGPT对话进行分析，发现超过三分之一的消息包含个人信息，且即使排除显性人口标识，LLM仍可高准确率推断用户年龄、性别和国籍，揭示了对话式AI中基于刻板模式的隐私泄露风险，指出单纯的PII删除不足以保护用户隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM助手用户数量急剧增长，用户的隐私泄露风险日益严峻。研究旨在量化和分析对话中个人信息的显性披露程度以及通过对话模式进行的隐性推断能力。

Method: 分析来自四个全球南部国家（巴西、印度、尼日利亚、巴基斯坦）1000多名用户的完整ChatGPT对话历史，使用LLM-based过滤器识别人口统计自我标识，并采用NER工具进行PII检测，随后使用现成LLM进行人口属性推断。

Result: 34.5%的用户消息包含个人信息（20类分类法），中位用户在对话历史的前14%即首次泄露身份信息；在排除显性人口统计标识的队列中，LLM仍能以加权F1 0.84、0.90、0.88的准确率推断年龄、性别和国家，中位用户仅凭前5%的对话即可被识别；并发现四类重复性刻板模式，其中错误分布不对称，主要集中在技术领域女性、技术成熟的老年用户和全球南部技术专业人员。

Conclusion: Message-level PII removal is insufficient as a standalone privacy intervention for conversational AI; ChatGPT serves as an effective inference surface competitive with traditional behavioral advertising platforms.

Abstract: Hundreds of millions of users now hold detailed, multi-turn conversations with ChatGPT and similar LLM assistants. We measure two privacy-relevant features of these conversations on a corpus of complete ChatGPT histories donated by over 1,000 users in four Global South countries (Brazil, India, Nigeria, Pakistan). First, on explicit disclosure: 34.5% of user messages contain personal information across a twenty-category taxonomy, with the median user first revealing identifying content within the first 14% of their conversation history. Second, on inference beyond explicit disclosure: we restrict to a cohort whose conversations contain no messages flagged by an LLM-based filter for explicit demographic self-identification (a separate NER pass marks PII for the disclosure audit but does not drive cohort exclusion). On this filtered cohort, an off the shelf large language model still recovers each user's age, gender, and country at weighted F1 of 0.84, 0.90, and 0.88, respectively, with the median user identified from the first 5% of their conversation history. Reading the model's natural-language reasoning traces, we identify four recurring stereotype patterns that drive both successful inference and an asymmetric error distribution concentrating on women in technical fields, older users with contemporary skills, and Global South tech professionals. We also compare ChatGPT against the same users' Google Search and YouTube histories as inference surfaces, and find it competitive with these older substrates that have driven behavioral advertising for two decades. Message-level PII removal is insufficient on its own as a privacy intervention for conversational AI data.

</details>


### [221] [Divergent Paths to Depolarization: Dialogue Design Determines the Prosocial Benefits of AI-Assisted Political Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23890)
*Jianlong Zhu,Syed Muhammad Jhon Raza Naqvi,Carolin-Theresa Ziemer,Usman Naseem,Ingmar Weber*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究探讨了人机对话格式对减少政治极化的影响。469名美国参与者就政治争议话题与AI聊天机器人进行两轮对话实验，发现态度一致对话产生更直接的极化缓解效果，而态度不一致对话的效益需要时间才能显现，强调对话设计是AI干预效果的关键决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治分歧中的论证对话可减少极化，但公民接触对立观点的机会有限。AI对话伙伴提供了可扩展的框架，但人机对话的具体格式如何影响其效益尚不明确。

Method: 两轮在线实验，469名美国参与者被分配就争议性政治议题支持或反对自己的态度与AI聊天机器人进行辩论。采用2×2实验设计（态度一致性×对话轮次）。

Result: 态度一致对话比态度不一致对话产生更显著的即时情感极化和观点极化缓解。态度不一致对话在即时认知状态共情上弱于非AI参考任务，但能在一周后提升认知特质共情水平。

Conclusion: 对话设计（格式）是决定AI介导行为干预有效性的关键因素。主动生成态度不一致论点可能产生延迟但持久的认知共情效益。

Abstract: Argumentative dialogues across political divides can reduce polarization, yet opportunities for citizens to engage with opposing views in accessible and structured ways remain limited. AI dialogue partners offer a scalable framework for such open-mindedness exercises, but how the format of human-AI dialogues shapes their benefits remains unclear. In a two-session online experiment, 469 US participants were assigned to argue either for or against their own attitude on a contested political issue with an AI chatbot. Our experimental findings show attitude-congruent dialogues produced greater immediate reduction in both affective and opinion polarization than attitude-incongruent dialogues. By contrast, attitude-incongruent dialogues elicited weaker cognitive state empathy than the non-AI reference task but increased cognitive trait empathy in the two-week period between sessions, suggesting the effects of active generation of attitude-incongruent arguments may emerge over time. These findings highlight dialogue design as a key determinant of effective AI-mediated behavioral interventions.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [How to Steer Your Multi-Agent System: Human-LLM Collaborative Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23023)
*Zeyu He,Hannah Kim,Dan Zhang,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体系统中人类难以管理复杂规划的问题，提出了一种沿模式、范围、层级三个维度的人机协作规划交互设计空间，并实现AMBIPOM原型支持过程级监督。用户研究和基准测试揭示了混合工作流模式及关键权衡关系，为设计更透明、可控的人机协作规划系统提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人机协作规划系统仅支持结果级监督，用户无法洞察中间推理过程，面对复杂计划时缺乏透明性和可控性。

Method: 沿三个轴（模式、范围、层级）形式化设计空间，实现AMBIPOM原型支持过程级监督；通过用户研究和受控基准测试进行评估。

Result: 揭示用户采用混合工作流并面临努力-控制-风险的权衡；分析了LLM在不同范围和策略下的计划修订表现。

Conclusion: 通过用户研究揭示混合工作流和权衡关系，通过基准测试分析LLM在不同条件下的计划修订行为，为构建更透明、可控、有效的人机协作规划系统提供设计指导。开源代码和数据以支持复现与后续研究。

Abstract: In orchestrated multi-agent systems, humans often struggle to manage plans due to their complexity and limited transparency. Existing approaches rely on outcome-level supervision, where users verify only final outputs without visibility into intermediate reasoning. We formalize a design space for human-LLM co-planning interactions along three axes: mode (semantic vs. structural), scope (global vs. targeted), and level (low vs. high-level edits). We realize it in AMBIPOM, a prototype supporting process-level supervision through both semantic and structural interactions. Through a user study, we characterize how users navigate this space, revealing hybrid workflows and effort-control-risk trade-offs; through a controlled benchmark, we analyze how LLMs revise plans under varying scope and revision strategies. Our findings yield design insights for more transparent, controllable, and effective human-AI co-planning. We release code and data at https://github.com/megagonlabs/ambipom.

</details>


### [223] [SVR-MAD: A Bayesian-Inspired Framework for Posterior-Guided Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23099)
*Weifan Jiang,Rana Shahout,Minghao Li,Zhenting Qi,Yilun Du,Michael Mitzenmacher,Minlan Yu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: SVR-MAD通过贝叶斯方法改进多智能体辩论系统，利用辩论前信号作为先验、辩论结果作为后验证据，逐步构建通信图，在减少61% token开销的同时保持或提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAD方法存在上下文快速增长、token开销过高的问题，且基于token级似然或LLM自报告置信度的剪枝信号在幻觉场景下不可靠，导致多智能体辩论的可扩展性受限。

Method: 提出SVR-MAD框架，将辩论前信号视为先验概率，将辩论结果视为后验式证据，用以估计智能体正确性；基于该证据增量构建通信图，优先保留答案经同行挑战存活的智能体。

Result: 在多个LLM和基准测试上，SVR-MAD将token成本降低最多61%，且准确率与最精确的对比MAD基线持平或更优。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯启发的证据累积方法能有效识别高价值通信，在降低计算开销的同时提升MAD系统的可扩展性和准确性。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) improves LLM-agent accuracy but suffers from rapid context growth, limiting scalability in larger multi-agent settings. Existing methods prune low-utility communications using prior signals, such as token-level log-likelihoods or LLM self-reported confidence. However, these signals become unreliable under hallucination, degrading the accuracy of MAD methods that rely on them. We propose SVR-MAD, a Bayesian-inspired MAD framework that treats pre-debate signals as priors and debate outcomes as posterior-style evidence for estimating agent correctness. SVR-MAD uses this evidence to incrementally construct the communication graph, prioritizing agents whose answers survive peer challenges. Experiments across multiple LLMs and benchmarks show that SVR-MAD reduces token cost by up to 61% while matching or improving accuracy relative to the most accurate competing MAD baseline.

</details>


### [224] [Self-Refining Topology Optimization via an LLM-Based Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23273)
*Hyunjee Park,Hayoung Chung*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了TopOptAgents，一个基于多智能体大语言模型(LLM)的拓扑优化自动化框架，通过6个协作智能体实现问题 formulation、验证、代码生成和设计质量评估的全流程自动化，并利用迭代自我改进机制纠正错误和优化设计。该方法在文献稀疏的优化问题上表现尤为突出，扩展了LLM自动化拓扑优化的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑优化工作流程中，工程师需要在数值参数设置、设计可行性评估等方面做出大量决策，这些决策依赖领域专业知识，阻碍了自动化设计流程的实现。现有方法难以在优化过程中自动处理这些决策问题。

Method: 研究提出了TopOptAgents多智能体系统，包含6个基于LLM的智能体，通过迭代自我改进循环进行协作。系统覆盖拓扑优化的关键阶段：问题formulation、验证、代码生成与执行、以及优化结构质量评估。通过多轮反馈实现错误纠正和设计渐进式改进。

Result: 实验选取了不同文献覆盖度和数值特征的优化问题进行验证。结果表明，对于预训练语言模型先前知识有限的问题类别（如文献和开源实现较少的问题 formulation），迭代自我改进的优势尤为显著。在这些情况下，该框架能够可靠地产生收敛设计，而单一先进LLM则难以完成。

Conclusion: 自我改进机制拓宽了LLM自动化可可靠解决的拓扑优化问题范围，为拓扑优化全流程自动化提供了有效途径，特别是在缺乏充分先验知识的复杂或新兴问题领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Topology optimization is a widely used design method that produces optimized material distributions for prescribed objectives and constraints through well-established numerical algorithms. Throughout the workflow, engineers make a series of decisions ranging from setting and adjusting numerical parameters to assessing whether the converged design meets considerations beyond those explicitly included in the optimization problem, such as physical feasibility. These decisions, which draw on domain expertise, interfere with the autonomous design process. To address this difficulty, this study presents TopOptAgents, a multi-agent system for automating not only the design process but also decision-making during the key stages of the topology optimization process. TopOptAgents consists of six LLM-based agents collaborating through iterative self-refinement cycles spanning problem formulation, validation, code generation and execution, and quality assessment of the optimized structure. This process enables error correction and progressive improvement of both the optimization setup and resulting design. The framework is demonstrated on optimization problems selected to cover a range of settings that differ in their literature coverage and numerical characteristics The benefits of iterative self-refinement are found to be particularly pronounced for problem classes where the pretrained language model has limited prior exposure, such as formulations whose literature and open-source implementations are comparatively sparse. In such cases, the proposed framework reliably produces converged designs where a single state-of-the-art LLM struggles, suggesting that self-refinement broadens the range of topology optimization problems that LLM-based automation can reliably address.

</details>


### [225] [ARMS: Automatic Reward Shaping for Sparse-Reward Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23562)
*Elie Abboud,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: ARMS是一个自监督的多智能体奖励塑造框架，通过轨迹排序将稀疏奖励转化为密集塑造信号，其核心创新在于基于条件最优响应推理的博弈论分析，确保奖励塑造保持纳什均衡特性，同时识别并解决了MARL中探索不足导致的振荡学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习（MARL）中稀疏奖励是主要瓶颈，同时学习导致非平稳性，奖励设计困难。现有奖励塑造方法必须保持问题的博弈结构而非仅改善短期优化。

Method: 提出ARMS框架，通过轨迹排序从稀疏环境奖励中学习密集塑造信号，交替进行策略学习和奖励学习，并在智能体间共享塑造参数以提高效率。理论层面，将策略不变性重新表述为条件最优响应推理，证明了在特定条件下塑造奖励可保持固定对手策略下各智能体的最优响应集。

Result: 实验表明ARMS在部分可观测多智能体路径规划任务中，提升了随奖励稀疏度和智能体数量增加时的采样效率，并能泛化到未见环境。发现了MARL特有的振荡行为失败模式，可通过增加探索来缓解和稳定学习。

Conclusion: ARMS是首个基于博弈论均衡保持理论支撑的多智能体自动奖励塑造框架，为MARL中的稀疏奖励问题提供了理论严谨的解决方案。

Abstract: Sparse rewards are a major bottleneck in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), where simultaneous learning induces non-stationarity and makes reward design especially delicate. Reward shaping can accelerate learning, but in the multi-agent setting it must preserve the strategic structure of the problem rather than merely improve short-term optimization. We propose Automatic Reward-shaping in Multi-agent Systems (ARMS), a self-supervised reward shaping framework for MARL that learns dense shaping signals from sparse environmental rewards through trajectory ranking. Since single-agent trajectory-ranking guarantees do not directly transfer to MARL, we reformulate policy invariance through conditional best-response reasoning, and show that if certain conditions hold, then using shaping rewards preserves each agent's best-response set under fixed opponent policies, and consequently preserve the set of Nash equilibria. Guided by this perspective, ARMS alternates between policy learning and reward learning while sharing shaping parameters across agents for efficiency. Experiments in a partially observable multi-agent pathfinding domain show that ARMS improves sampling efficiency under increasing reward sparsity and agent count, generalizes to unseen environments, and reveals a MARL-specific failure mode in which limited exploration and coupled policy--reward dynamics induce oscillatory behavior. Increasing exploration mitigates this effect and stabilizes learning. To the best of our knowledge, ARMS is the first automatic reward shaping framework for MARL whose design is motivated by a game-theoretic equilibrium-preservation result.

</details>


### [226] [Safety, Liveness, and Fairness in Quantitative Argumentation Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23578)
*Arunavo Ganguly,Julian Alfredo Mendez,Timotheus Kampik*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文将时序推理中的安全性、活跃性和公平性概念引入到带有加权节点和图更新的定量（双极）论证对话框架中，研究论证强度在论证阈值附近的动态行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证系统需要处理动态更新的论证图，但现有研究缺乏对论证强度随时间演变的系统性分析框架，特别是在论证强度是否持续高于/低于阈值、是否最终达到阈值等关键性质方面缺乏形式化定义和理论保证。

Method: 1. 定义强安全性(Strong Safety)和弱安全性(Weak Safety)：分别表示论证强度在整个过程中始终保持在阈值以上，以及最终能达阈值；2. 定义活跃性(Liveness)：表示论证强度在阈值附近波动；3. 定义公平性(Fairness)：评估论证图中安全性论证的分布情况；4. 形式化证明这些性质之间的相互关系。

Result: 证明了安全性、活跃性和公平性等概念之间的形式化关系，同时指出为这些性质提供一般性保证存在分析层面的挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作为研究动态论证系统的时间演化行为提供了理论基础，但一般性保证难以获得，提示未来研究需结合具体应用场景进行分析。

Abstract: We introduce notions of safety, liveness, and fairness, as commonly used in temporal reasoning, to quantitative (bipolar) argumentation dialogues where repeated inferences are drawn from argumentation graphs with weighted nodes. Between inferences, these graphs undergo updates. Strong and weak safety capture that arguments' (final) strengths remain above a specific threshold of justification and always reach the threshold eventually, respectively. Liveness requires that arguments' strengths fluctuate across the threshold of justification. Fairness notions assess how safe arguments are spread within a sequence of argumentation graphs. We formally show how these notions are related, and discuss some analytical challenges with respect to providing general guarantees for our properties.

</details>


### [227] [The Communication Complexity of Instant-Runoff Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23743)
*Élie de Panafieu,François Durand,Jérôme Lang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Resolved the communication complexity of Instant-Runoff Voting (IRV) by proving matching upper and lower bounds of Θ(n (log m)²) using the fooling set technique. Also showed reduced complexity under single-peakedness and same complexity for IRV-Average and STV variants.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conitzer and Sandholm [2005] provided O(n (log m)²) upper bound for IRV communication complexity, but could only prove Ω(n log m) lower bound, leaving a gap to be resolved.

Method: Fooling set technique (communication complexity lower bound method)

Result: Lower bound raised from Ω(n log m) to Ω(n (log m)²), exactly matching the existing upper bound. Under single-peakedness restriction, complexity reduces to Θ(n log m). Both IRV-Average and STV maintain the same asymptotic complexity as IRV.

Conclusion: Communication complexity of IRV is Θ(n (log m)²). Under single-peakedness, complexity reduces to Θ(n log m). IRV-Average and STV share the same asymptotic complexity as IRV.

Abstract: The communication complexity of a voting rule is the worst-case number of bits that n voters must transmit to a central authority under the most efficient elicitation protocol in an election with m candidates. We study the communication complexity of Instant-Runoff Voting (IRV). Conitzer and Sandholm [2005] established an upper bound of O(n (log m)${}^2$), but did not provide a matching lower bound beyond $Ω$(n log m). We resolve this open problem by raising the lower bound to $Ω$(n (log m)${}^2$) using the fooling set technique, thereby showing that the communication complexity of IRV is $Θ$(n (log m)${}^2$). We further show that this complexity drops to $Θ$(n log m) under the single-peakedness restriction, and that both the IRV-Average variant and Single Transferable Vote (STV), the multiwinner extension of IRV, have the same asymptotic communication complexity as IRV.

</details>
